Implement the following plan:

# Asset Panel with Drag-Drop to Timeline

## Context
Currently, assets can only be added to the timeline by editing `timeline.yaml` manually. The user wants a panel showing available assets with drag-drop support to add clips to the timeline visually.

## Overview
Add a right-side asset panel that lists assets from `asset_map`, defaults to showing only unused ones, and supports dragging assets onto the timeline to create new clips.

## Files to Modify

| File | Change |
|------|--------|
| `viewer/src/AssetPanel.tsx` | **NEW** — Draggable asset list component |
| `viewer/src/App.tsx` | Layout restructure, drop handlers, `handleAssetDrop` |
| `viewer/src/App.css` | Panel styles, layout changes, drop indicator |
| `viewer/src/timeline-data.ts` | Add `inferTrackType()` and `getNextClipId()` utilities |

## Implementation Steps

### 1. Add utilities to `timeline-data.ts`

- `inferTrackType(filePath)` — maps file extension to `video`/`audio`/`overlay`
  - `.mp4`, `.webm`, `.mov` → `video`
  - `.mp3`, `.wav`, `.aac`, `.m4a`, `.MP3` → `audio`
  - `.jpg`, `.jpeg`, `.png`, `.gif`, `.svg` → `overlay`
- `getNextClipId(meta)` — scans existing `clip-N` keys, returns `clip-{max+1}`

### 2. Create `AssetPanel.tsx`

Props: `assetMap`, `rows`, `meta`, `backgroundAsset?`

Behavior:
- Compute used assets: scan all row actions → collect `meta[action.id].asset` into a Set
- Toggle between "unused only" (default) and "show all"
- Exclude the background asset (e.g. `bg-dark-abstract`)
- Each item is `draggable`, sets `dataTransfer` with asset key + inferred track type
- Show: asset name, type badge (VIDEO/AUDIO/IMAGE), colored left border via `getAssetColor()`

### 3. Restructure layout in `App.tsx` + `App.css`

Current:
```
.video-container → LivePreview + OverlayEditor (centered)
```

New:
```
.video-container (flex-direction: row)
  └─ .preview-section (flex: 1, centers preview + overlay)
  └─ AssetPanel (width: 200px, right side)
```

Move `LivePreview` + `OverlayEditor` inside a new `.preview-section` wrapper.

### 4. Add drop handlers on `.timeline-wrapper`

- `onDragOver`: `e.preventDefault()` + add `.drag-over` class for visual feedback
- `onDragLeave`: remove `.drag-over` class
- `onDrop`: extract asset key + track type from `dataTransfer`, compute timeline time from drop X position

**Time calculation:**
```
pixelsPerSecond = scaleWidth / scale = 160 / 5 = 32
time = (dropX - startLeft + scrollLeft) / pixelsPerSecond
```

### 5. Implement `handleAssetDrop(assetKey, trackType, time)`

1. Generate clip ID via `getNextClipId(meta)`
2. Create `ClipMeta` with defaults:
   - video: `from: 0, to: 5, speed: 1, x: 0, y: 0, width: 1280, height: 720`
   - audio: `from: 0, to: 10, speed: 1, volume: 1`
   - overlay: `from: 0, to: 0, hold: 5, x: 100, y: 100, width: 320, height: 240, opacity: 1`
3. Create `TimelineAction`: `{ id, start: time, end: time + duration, effectId }`
4. Find target row (or create new `row-audio-N` for new audio assets)
5. Ensure effect exists in `data.effects`
6. **Fix `applyTimelineEdit`**: currently only patches existing meta — add `else` branch to create new entries
7. Call `applyTimelineEdit(newRows, { [clipId]: fullClipMeta })`

### 6. Fix `applyTimelineEdit` to support new clips

Current code (line 110): `if (next[id]) { next[id] = { ...next[id], ...patch } }`

Change to also handle creation: `next[id] = next[id] ? { ...next[id], ...patch } : patch as ClipMeta`

## Verification
1. Run `npm run dev` in `viewer/`
2. Asset panel appears on right with unused assets listed
3. Toggle shows all assets including used ones
4. Drag an asset onto the timeline → new clip appears at drop position
5. Clip auto-saves to YAML (check `timeline.yaml`)
6. Preview renders the new clip correctly

1→import yaml from 'js-yaml'
     2→import type { TimelineAction, TimelineEffect, TimelineRow } from '@xzdarcy/react-timeline-editor'
     3→
     4→export interface ClipMeta {
     5→  asset: string
     6→  track: 'video' | 'audio' | 'overlay'
     7→  from: number
     8→  to: number
     9→  speed: number
    10→  volume: number
    11→  x: number
    12→  y: number
    13→  width: number
    14→  height: number

    16→}
    17→
    18→interface RawClip {
    19→  at: number
    20→  track: 'video' | 'audio' | 'overlay'
    21→  asset: string
    22→  from: number
    23→  to: number
    24→  speed?: number
    25→  volume?: number
    26→  hold?: number
    27→  x?: number
    28→  y?: number
    29→  width?: number
    30→  height?: number
    31→  opacity?: number
    32→}
    33→
    34→interface RawTimeline {
    35→  output: { resolution: string; fps: number; file: string; background?: string; background_scale?: number }
    36→  asset_map: Record<string, string>
    37→  clips: RawClip[]
    38→}
    39→
    40→// Colors per asset
    41→const ASSET_COLORS: Record<string, string> = {
    42→  'output-composition': '#e06c75',
    43→  'venn-diagram': '#61afef',
    44→  'demo-one': '#98c379',
    45→  'demo-two': '#c678dd',
    46→  'example-video': '#56b6c2',
    47→  'example-image1': '#d19a66',
    48→  'example-image2': '#e5c07b',
    49→  'input': '#61afef',
    50→}
    51→
    52→export function getAssetColor(asset: string): string {
    53→  return ASSET_COLORS[asset] ?? '#abb2bf'
    54→}
    55→
    56→// Compute source media time from timeline time
    57→export function getSourceTime(clip: { from: number; start: number; speed: number }, time: number): number {
    58→  return clip.from + (time - clip.start) * clip.speed
    59→}
    60→
    61→export interface TranscriptSegment {
    62→  start: number  // source audio time
    63→  end: number
    64→  text: string
    65→}
    66→
    67→export interface TimelineData {
    68→  rows: TimelineRow[]
    69→  meta: Record<string, ClipMeta>
    70→  effects: Record<string, TimelineEffect>
    71→  assetMap: Record<string, string>
    72→  header: { output: RawTimeline['output']; asset_map: RawTimeline['asset_map'] }
    73→}
    74→
    75→// Load transcript segments from asset profile
    76→export async function loadTranscript(assetKey: string, assetPath: string): Promise<TranscriptSegment[]> {
    77→  const filename = assetPath.split('/').pop() ?? ''
    78→  const ext = filename.includes('.') ? filename.slice(filename.lastIndexOf('.')) : ''
    79→
    80→  // Try filename first, then asset key + extension
    81→  for (const name of [filename, `${assetKey}${ext}`]) {
    82→    const res = await fetch(`/media/asset_profiles/${name}.yaml`)
    83→    if (!res.ok) continue
    84→    const text = await res.text()
    85→    const profile = yaml.load(text) as { transcript?: { segments?: TranscriptSegment[] } }
    86→    const segments = profile?.transcript?.segments
    87→    if (segments?.length) return segments
    88→  }
    89→  return []
    90→}
    91→
    92→function buildFromRaw(timeline: RawTimeline): TimelineData {
    93→  const effects: Record<string, TimelineEffect> = {}
    94→  for (const clip of timeline.clips) {
    95→    const effectId = `effect-${clip.asset}`
    96→    if (!effects[effectId]) {
    97→      effects[effectId] = { id: effectId }
    98→    }
    99→  }
   100→
   101→  const videoActions: TimelineAction[] = []
   102→  const audioRowMap: Record<string, TimelineAction[]> = {}
   103→  const overlayActions: TimelineAction[] = []
   104→  const meta: Record<string, ClipMeta> = {}
   105→
   106→  // Track audio row assignment: each unique asset gets its own row
   107→  let audioRowCounter = 0
   108→  const assetToAudioRow: Record<string, string> = {}
   109→
   110→  for (const [i, clip] of timeline.clips.entries()) {
   111→    const speed = clip.speed ?? 1
   112→    let duration: number
   113→    if (clip.track === 'overlay' && clip.hold != null) {
   114→      duration = clip.hold
   115→    } else {
   116→      duration = (clip.to - clip.from) / speed
   117→    }
   118→    const action: TimelineAction = {
   119→      id: `clip-${i}`,
   120→      start: clip.at,
   121→      end: clip.at + duration,
   122→      effectId: `effect-${clip.asset}`,
   123→    }
   124→    meta[action.id] = {
   125→      asset: clip.asset,
   126→      track: clip.track,
   127→      from: clip.from ?? 0,
   128→      to: clip.to ?? (clip.hold ?? 0),
   129→      speed,
   130→      volume: clip.volume ?? 1.0,
   131→      x: clip.x ?? 0,
   132→      y: clip.y ?? 0,
   133→      width: clip.width ?? 320,
   134→      height: clip.height ?? 240,
   135→      opacity: clip.opacity ?? 1.0,
   136→    }
   137→    if (clip.track === 'video') {
   138→      videoActions.push(action)
   139→    } else if (clip.track === 'overlay') {
   140→      overlayActions.push(action)
   141→    } else {
   142→      // Assign each audio asset to its own row for multi-track support
   143→      if (!assetToAudioRow[clip.asset]) {
   144→        assetToAudioRow[clip.asset] = `row-audio-${audioRowCounter++}`
   145→      }
   146→      const rowId = assetToAudioRow[clip.asset]
   147→      if (!audioRowMap[rowId]) audioRowMap[rowId] = []
   148→      audioRowMap[rowId].push(action)
   149→    }
   150→  }
   151→
   152→  const audioRows: TimelineRow[] = Object.entries(audioRowMap).map(
   153→    ([id, actions]) => ({ id, actions })
   154→  )
   155→
   156→  return {
   157→    rows: [
   158→      { id: 'row-video', actions: videoActions },
   159→      ...audioRows,
   160→      { id: 'row-overlay', actions: overlayActions },
   161→    ],
   162→    meta,
   163→    effects,
   164→    assetMap: timeline.asset_map,
   165→    header: { output: timeline.output, asset_map: timeline.asset_map },
   166→  }
   167→}
   168→
   169→// Parse yaml text into timeline data
   170→export function parseTimeline(text: string): TimelineData {
   171→  const timeline = yaml.load(text) as RawTimeline
   172→  return buildFromRaw(timeline)
   173→}
   174→
   175→// Convert editor state back to YAML string
   176→export function toYaml(
   177→  rows: TimelineRow[],
   178→  meta: Record<string, ClipMeta>,
   179→  header: TimelineData['header'],
   180→): string {
   181→  const clips: RawClip[] = []
   182→
   183→  function rowTrack(rowId: string): 'video' | 'audio' | 'overlay' | null {
   184→    if (rowId === 'row-video') return 'video'
   185→    if (rowId.startsWith('row-audio')) return 'audio'
   186→    if (rowId === 'row-overlay') return 'overlay'
   187→    return null
   188→  }
   189→
   190→  for (const row of rows) {
   191→    const track = rowTrack(row.id)
   192→    if (!track) continue
   193→    const sorted = [...row.actions].sort((a, b) => a.start - b.start)
   194→    for (const action of sorted) {
   195→      const m = meta[action.id]
   196→      if (!m) continue
   197→
   198→      if (track === 'overlay') {
   199→        const clip: RawClip = {
   200→          at: round(action.start),
   201→          track,
   202→          asset: m.asset,
   203→          from: 0,
   204→          to: 0,
   205→          hold: round(action.end - action.start),
   206→          x: round(m.x),
   207→          y: round(m.y),
   208→          width: round(m.width),
   209→          height: round(m.height),
   210→          opacity: round(m.opacity),
   211→        }
   212→        clips.push(clip)
   213→      } else {
   214→        const clip: RawClip = {
   215→          at: round(action.start),
   216→          track,
   217→          asset: m.asset,
   218→          from: round(m.from),
   219→          to: round(getSourceTime({ from: m.from, start: action.start, speed: m.speed }, action.end)),
   220→        }
   221→        if (m.speed !== 1) {
   222→          clip.speed = round(m.speed)
   223→        }
   224→        if (track === 'audio' && m.volume !== 1) {
   225→          clip.volume = round(m.volume)
   226→        }
   227→        clips.push(clip)
   228→      }
   229→    }
   230→  }
   231→
   232→  const doc = {
   233→    output: header.output,
   234→    asset_map: header.asset_map,
   235→    clips,
   236→  }
   237→
   238→  return yaml.dump(doc, {
   239→    lineWidth: -1,
   240→    noRefs: true,
   241→    quotingType: '"',
   242→    forceQuotes: false,
   243→  })
   244→}
   245→
   246→function round(n: number): number {
   247→  return Math.round(n * 100) / 100
   248→}
   249→
```

    20→function getRowLabel(rowId: string, meta: Record<string, ClipMeta>, rows: TimelineRow[]): string {
    21→  if (rowId === 'row-video') return 'Video'
    22→  if (rowId === 'row-overlay') return 'Overlay'
    23→  if (rowId.startsWith('row-audio')) {
    24→    // Find the asset name for this audio row
    25→    const row = rows.find(r => r.id === rowId)
    26→    if (row && row.actions.length > 0) {
    27→      const m = meta[row.actions[0].id]
    28→      if (m) return m.asset
    29→    }
    30→    return 'Audio'
    31→  }
    32→  return rowId
    33→}

    35→function App() {
    36→  const timelineRef = useRef<TimelineState>(null)
    37→  const previewRef = useRef<PreviewHandle>(null)
    38→  const canvasRef = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement | null>(null)
    39→  const isSyncingFromPreview = useRef(false)

    43→  // Runtime-loaded timeline data

    45→  const [rows, setRows] = useState<TimelineRow[]>([])
    46→  const [meta, setMeta] = useState<Record<string, ClipMeta>>({})
    47→  const [transcript, setTranscript] = useState<TranscriptSegment[]>([])
    48→  const [saveStatus, setSaveStatus] = useState<'saved' | 'saving' | 'dirty' | 'error'>('saved')
    49→  const [renderStatus, setRenderStatus] = useState<'idle' | 'rendering' | 'done' | 'error'>('idle')
    50→  const [renderLog, setRenderLog] = useState('')
    51→  const [renderDirty, setRenderDirty] = useState(false)
    52→  const saveTimer = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null)
    53→  const headerRef = useRef<TimelineData['header'] | null>(null)
    54→
    55→  // Ref always holds the latest meta, so save callbacks don't need it as a dep
    56→  const metaRef = useRef(meta)
    57→  metaRef.current = meta
    58→
    59→  // Suppress onChange saves during poll-triggered state updates.
    60→  const ignoreNextOnChange = useRef(false)
    61→  useEffect(() => {
    62→    ignoreNextOnChange.current = false
    63→  })
    64→
    65→  // ── Single update path for all timeline edits ─────────────────────────────
    66→  // Every edit (drag, resize, poll) flows through here.
    67→
    68→  const saveToYaml = useCallback(async (currentRows: TimelineRow[]) => {
    69→    if (!headerRef.current) return
    70→    setSaveStatus('saving')
    71→    try {
    72→      const yamlStr = toYaml(currentRows, metaRef.current, headerRef.current)
    73→      lastYaml.current = yamlStr
    74→      const res = await fetch('/api/save-timeline', {
    75→        method: 'POST',
    76→        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'text/yaml' },
    77→        body: yamlStr,
    78→      })
    79→      if (res.ok) {
    80→        setSaveStatus('saved')
    81→        setRenderDirty(true)
    82→      } else {
    83→        setSaveStatus('error')
    84→      }
    85→    } catch {
    86→      setSaveStatus('error')
    87→    }
    88→  }, [])
    89→
    90→  const scheduleSave = useCallback((currentRows: TimelineRow[]) => {
    91→    setSaveStatus('dirty')
    92→    if (saveTimer.current) clearTimeout(saveTimer.current)
    93→    saveTimer.current = setTimeout(() => saveToYaml(currentRows), 500)
    94→  }, [saveToYaml])
    95→
    96→  /**
    97→   * Central update function. All edits to rows/meta go through here.
    98→   * Ensures state is set, save is scheduled, and preview is refreshed.
    99→   */
   100→  const applyTimelineEdit = useCallback((
   101→    newRows: TimelineRow[],
   102→    metaUpdates?: Record<string, Partial<ClipMeta>>,
   103→  ) => {
   104→    setRows(newRows)
   105→
   106→    if (metaUpdates && Object.keys(metaUpdates).length > 0) {
   107→      setMeta(prev => {
   108→        const next = { ...prev }
   109→        for (const [id, patch] of Object.entries(metaUpdates)) {
   110→          if (next[id]) {
   111→            next[id] = { ...next[id], ...patch }
   112→          }
   113→        }
   114→        return next
   115→      })
   116→    }
   117→
   118→    scheduleSave(newRows)
   119→    // Refresh preview after React commits the new state
   120→    queueMicrotask(() => previewRef.current?.refresh())
   121→  }, [scheduleSave])
   122→
   123→  // Keep canvasRef in sync with the preview's canvas element
   124→  useEffect(() => {

   127→
   128→  // ── Poll for external yaml changes ────────────────────────────────────────
   129→
   130→  const lastYaml = useRef('')
   131→  useEffect(() => {
   132→    const poll = async () => {
   133→      try {
   134→        const res = await fetch('/api/timeline')
   135→        const text = await res.text()
   136→        if (text !== lastYaml.current) {
   137→          lastYaml.current = text
   138→          const d = parseTimeline(text)
   139→          ignoreNextOnChange.current = true
   140→          setData(d)

   148→    poll()
   149→    const id = setInterval(poll, 1000)
   150→    return () => clearInterval(id)
   151→  }, [])
   152→
   153→  // Load transcript once when data first available
   154→  const transcriptLoaded = useRef(false)
   155→  useEffect(() => {

   158→    if (!audioMeta) return

   160→    if (!assetPath) return
   161→    transcriptLoaded.current = true
   162→    loadTranscript(audioMeta.asset, assetPath).then(setTranscript)
   163→  }, [data])
   164→
   165→  // ── Preview ↔ Timeline cursor sync ────────────────────────────────────────
   166→
   167→  const onPreviewTimeUpdate = useCallback((time: number) => {
   168→    if (isSyncingFromTimeline.current) return

   186→
   187→  const onClickTimeArea = useCallback((time: number) => {
   188→    previewRef.current?.seek(time)

   190→    return undefined
   191→  }, [])
   192→
   193→  // ── Timeline editor callbacks (all flow through applyTimelineEdit) ────────
   194→
   195→  // Track start positions before resize begins
   196→  const resizeStartRef = useRef<Record<string, { start: number; end: number; from: number }>>({})
   197→
   198→  const onActionResizeStart = useCallback(({ action }: { action: TimelineAction; row: TimelineRow; dir: string }) => {
   199→    const m = metaRef.current[action.id]
   200→    if (!m) return
   201→    resizeStartRef.current[action.id] = { start: action.start, end: action.end, from: m.from }
   202→  }, [])
   203→
   204→  const onActionResizeEnd = useCallback(({ action, row, dir }: { action: TimelineAction; row: TimelineRow; dir: string; start: number; end: number }) => {
   205→    const m = metaRef.current[action.id]
   206→    const metaUpdates: Record<string, Partial<ClipMeta>> = {}
   207→
   208→    if (dir === 'left') {
   209→      const orig = resizeStartRef.current[action.id]
   210→      if (orig && m) {
   211→        const startDelta = action.start - orig.start
   212→        metaUpdates[action.id] = {
   213→          from: Math.max(0, orig.from + startDelta * m.speed),
   214→        }

   216→    } else if (dir === 'right' && m) {
   217→      metaUpdates[action.id] = {
   218→        to: getSourceTime({ from: m.from, start: action.start, speed: m.speed }, action.end),
   219→      }
   220→    }
   221→
   222→    // Build updated rows with the final action positions
   223→    const newRows = rows.map(r => {
   224→      if (r.id !== row.id) return r
   225→      return {
   226→        ...r,
   227→        actions: r.actions.map(a =>
   228→          a.id === action.id ? { ...a, start: action.start, end: action.end } : a
   229→        ),
   230→      }
   231→    })
   232→
   233→    applyTimelineEdit(newRows, metaUpdates)
   234→    delete resizeStartRef.current[action.id]
   235→  }, [rows, applyTimelineEdit])
   236→
   237→  // Handle data changes from drag/move
   238→  const onChange = useCallback((newRows: TimelineRow[]) => {
   239→    if (ignoreNextOnChange.current) return
   240→    applyTimelineEdit(newRows)
   241→  }, [applyTimelineEdit])
   242→
   243→  // ── Overlay editing ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   244→
   245→  const onOverlayChange = useCallback((actionId: string, patch: Partial<ClipMeta>) => {
   246→    applyTimelineEdit(rows, { [actionId]: patch })
   247→  }, [rows, applyTimelineEdit])
   248→
   249→  // ── Rendering ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   250→
   251→  const getActionRender = useCallback((action: TimelineAction, _row: TimelineRow) => {
   252→    const m = meta[action.id]
   253→    if (!m) return null
   254→    const color = getAssetColor(m.asset)
   255→    const srcEnd = getSourceTime({ from: m.from, start: action.start, speed: m.speed }, action.end)
   256→    return (
   257→      <div
   258→        className="clip-action"
   259→        style={{ backgroundColor: color }}
   260→      >
   261→        <span className="clip-label">{m.asset}</span>
   262→        <span className="clip-time">
   263→          src {m.from.toFixed(1)}s - {srcEnd.toFixed(1)}s
   264→        </span>
   265→      </div>
   266→    )
   267→  }, [meta])
   268→
   269→  // Keyboard: space to toggle play
   270→  useEffect(() => {
   271→    const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
   272→      if (e.code === 'Space' && e.target === document.body) {
   273→        e.preventDefault()
   274→        previewRef.current?.togglePlayPause()
   275→      }
   276→    }
   277→    window.addEventListener('keydown', onKey)
   278→    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey)
   279→  }, [])
   280→
   281→  const startRender = useCallback(async () => {
   282→    if (renderStatus === 'rendering') return
   283→    setRenderStatus('rendering')
   284→    setRenderLog('')
   285→    try {
   286→      const res = await fetch('/api/render', { method: 'POST' })
   287→      const reader = res.body?.getReader()
   288→      if (!reader) { setRenderStatus('error'); return }
   289→      const decoder = new TextDecoder()
   290→      let buf = ''
   291→      while (true) {
   292→        const { done, value } = await reader.read()
   293→        if (done) break
   294→        buf += decoder.decode(value, { stream: true })
   295→        const lines = buf.split('\n')
   296→        buf = lines.pop() ?? ''
   297→        for (const line of lines) {
   298→          if (line.startsWith('event: done')) {
   299→            setRenderStatus('done')
   300→            setRenderDirty(false)
   301→          } else if (line.startsWith('event: error')) {
   302→            setRenderStatus('error')
   303→          } else if (line.startsWith('data: ')) {
   304→            setRenderLog(prev => prev + line.slice(6) + '\n')
   305→          }
   306→        }
   307→      }
   308→    } catch {
   309→      setRenderStatus('error')
   310→    }
   311→  }, [renderStatus])
   312→
   313→  const formatTime = (t: number) => {
   314→    const mins = Math.floor(t / 60)
   315→    const secs = Math.floor(t % 60)
   316→    const ms = Math.floor((t % 1) * 100)
   317→    return `${mins}:${secs.toString().padStart(2, '0')}.${ms.toString().padStart(2, '0')}`
   318→  }
   319→
   320→  const statusIndicator = {
   321→    saved: '●', saving: '◌', dirty: '○', error: '✕',
   322→  }[saveStatus]
   323→
   324→  const statusColor = {
   325→    saved: '#98c379', saving: '#e5c07b', dirty: '#e5c07b', error: '#e06c75',
   326→  }[saveStatus]
   327→
   328→  if (!data) {
   329→    return <div className="app" style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }}>Loading timeline...</div>
   330→  }
   331→
   332→  return (
   333→    <div className="app">
   334→      <div className="top-bar">
   335→        <h1>Timeline Viewer</h1>
   336→        <div className="top-bar-right">
   337→          <button
   338→            className={`render-btn${renderDirty ? ' render-dirty' : ''}${renderStatus === 'rendering' ? ' rendering' : ''}`}
   339→            onClick={startRender}
   340→            disabled={renderStatus === 'rendering'}
   341→            title={renderLog || undefined}
   342→          >
   343→            {renderStatus === 'rendering' ? 'Rendering...' : renderDirty ? 'Render' : 'Render'}
   344→          </button>
   345→          <span className="save-status" style={{ color: statusColor }}>
   346→            {statusIndicator} {saveStatus}
   347→          </span>
   348→          <span className="timecode">{formatTime(currentTime)}</span>
   349→        </div>
   350→      </div>
   351→
   352→      <div className="video-container">
   353→        <LivePreview
   354→          ref={previewRef}
   355→          rows={rows}
   356→          meta={meta}
   357→          assetPaths={data.assetMap}
   358→          transcript={transcript}
   359→          background={data.header.output.background ? data.assetMap[data.header.output.background] : undefined}
   360→          backgroundScale={data.header.output.background_scale}
   361→          onTimeUpdate={onPreviewTimeUpdate}
   362→        />
   363→        <OverlayEditor
   364→          rows={rows}
   365→          meta={meta}
   366→          currentTime={currentTime}
   367→          canvasRef={canvasRef}
   368→          backgroundScale={data.header.output.background_scale ?? 1}
   369→          onOverlayChange={onOverlayChange}
   370→        />
   371→      </div>
   372→
   373→      <div className="timeline-container">
   374→        <div className="track-labels">
   375→          {rows.map((row) => (
   376→            <div key={row.id} className="track-label">
   377→              {getRowLabel(row.id, meta, rows)}
   378→            </div>
   379→          ))}
   380→        </div>
   381→        <div className="timeline-wrapper">
   382→          <Timeline
   383→            ref={timelineRef}
   384→            style={{ width: '100%', height: '100%' }}
   385→            editorData={rows}
   386→            effects={data.effects}
   387→            onChange={onChange}
   388→            scale={5}
   389→            scaleWidth={160}
   390→            scaleSplitCount={5}
   391→            startLeft={20}
   392→            rowHeight={40}
   393→            autoScroll={true}
   394→            dragLine={true}
   395→            getActionRender={getActionRender}
   396→            onCursorDrag={onCursorDrag}
   397→            onClickTimeArea={onClickTimeArea}
   398→            onActionResizeStart={onActionResizeStart}
   399→            onActionResizeEnd={onActionResizeEnd}
   400→          />
   401→        </div>
   402→      </div>
   403→    </div>
   404→  )
   405→}
   406→
   407→export default App
   408→
```

  "old_string": "function round(n: number): number {\n  return Math.round(n * 100) / 100\n}",
  "new_string": "function round(n: number): number {\n  return Math.round(n * 100) / 100\n}\n\nexport function inferTrackType(filePath: string): 'video' | 'audio' | 'overlay' {\n  const ext = filePath.slice(filePath.lastIndexOf('.')).toLowerCase()\n  if (['.mp4', '.webm', '.mov'].includes(ext)) return 'video'\n  if (['.mp3', '.wav', '.aac', '.m4a'].includes(ext)) return 'audio'\n  return 'overlay'\n}\n\nexport function getNextClipId(meta: Record<string, ClipMeta>): string {\n  let max = -1\n  for (const id of Object.keys(meta)) {\n    const match = id.match(/^clip-(\\d+)$/)\n    if (match) max = Math.max(max, parseInt(match[1], 10))\n  }\n  return `clip-${max + 1}`\n}"
}

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/announcement-video/viewer/src/AssetPanel.tsx",
  "content": "import { useState, useMemo } from 'react'\nimport type { TimelineRow } from '@xzdarcy/react-timeline-editor'\nimport { getAssetColor, inferTrackType, type ClipMeta } from './timeline-data'\n\ninterface AssetPanelProps {\n  assetMap: Record<string, string>\n  rows: TimelineRow[]\n  meta: Record<string, ClipMeta>\n  backgroundAsset?: string\n}\n\nexport default function AssetPanel({ assetMap, rows, meta, backgroundAsset }: AssetPanelProps) {\n  const [showAll, setShowAll] = useState(false)\n\n  const usedAssets = useMemo(() => {\n    const used = new Set<string>()\n    for (const row of rows) {\n      for (const action of row.actions) {\n        const m = meta[action.id]\n        if (m) used.add(m.asset)\n      }\n    }\n    return used\n  }, [rows, meta])\n\n  const assets = useMemo(() => {\n    return Object.entries(assetMap)\n      .filter(([key]) => key !== backgroundAsset)\n      .filter(([key]) => showAll || !usedAssets.has(key))\n      .map(([key, path]) => ({\n        key,\n        path,\n        trackType: inferTrackType(path),\n      }))\n  }, [assetMap, backgroundAsset, showAll, usedAssets])\n\n  const typeLabel = (t: string) => {\n    if (t === 'video') return 'VIDEO'\n    if (t === 'audio') return 'AUDIO'\n    return 'IMAGE'\n  }\n\n  const typeBadgeClass = (t: string) => `asset-type-badge asset-type-${t}`\n\n  const onDragStart = (e: React.DragEvent, key: string, trackType: string) => {\n    e.dataTransfer.setData('asset-key', key)\n    e.dataTransfer.setData('track-type', trackType)\n    e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = 'copy'\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"asset-panel\">\n      <div className=\"asset-panel-header\">\n        <span className=\"asset-panel-title\">Assets</span>\n        <button\n          className=\"asset-toggle-btn\"\n          onClick={() => setShowAll(!showAll)}\n        >\n          {showAll ? 'Unused' : 'All'}\n        </button>\n      </div>\n      <div className=\"asset-list\">\n        {assets.length === 0 && (\n          <div className=\"asset-empty\">No unused assets</div>\n        )}\n        {assets.map(({ key, trackType }) => (\n          <div\n            key={key}\n            className=\"asset-item\"\n            draggable\n            onDragStart={(e) => onDragStart(e, key, trackType)}\n            style={{ borderLeftColor: getAssetColor(key) }}\n          >\n            <span className=\"asset-name\">{key}</span>\n            <span className={typeBadgeClass(trackType)}>{typeLabel(trackType)}</span>\n          </div>\n        ))}\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  )\n}\n"
}
```

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/announcement-video/viewer/src/App.tsx",
  "old_string": "  // ── Rendering ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────",
  "new_string": "  // ── Asset drag-drop ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n  const handleAssetDrop = useCallback((assetKey: string, trackType: string, time: number) => {\n    if (!data) return\n    const clipId = getNextClipId(metaRef.current)\n\n    let clipMeta: ClipMeta\n    let duration: number\n    if (trackType === 'video') {\n      duration = 5\n      clipMeta = { asset: assetKey, track: 'video', from: 0, to: 5, speed: 1, volume: 1, x: 0, y: 0, width: 1280, height: 720, opacity: 1 }\n    } else if (trackType === 'audio') {\n      duration = 10\n      clipMeta = { asset: assetKey, track: 'audio', from: 0, to: 10, speed: 1, volume: 1, x: 0, y: 0, width: 0, height: 0, opacity: 1 }\n    } else {\n      duration = 5\n      clipMeta = { asset: assetKey, track: 'overlay', from: 0, to: 0, speed: 1, volume: 1, x: 100, y: 100, width: 320, height: 240, opacity: 1 }\n    }\n\n    const effectId = `effect-${assetKey}`\n    if (data.effects && !data.effects[effectId]) {\n      data.effects[effectId] = { id: effectId }\n    }\n\n    const action: TimelineAction = {\n      id: clipId,\n      start: time,\n      end: time + duration,\n      effectId,\n    }\n\n    let newRows: TimelineRow[]\n    if (trackType === 'video') {\n      newRows = rows.map(r =>\n        r.id === 'row-video' ? { ...r, actions: [...r.actions, action] } : r\n      )\n    } else if (trackType === 'overlay') {\n      newRows = rows.map(r =>\n        r.id === 'row-overlay' ? { ...r, actions: [...r.actions, action] } : r\n      )\n    } else {\n      // Audio: find existing row for this asset or create new one\n      const existingRow = rows.find(r =>\n        r.id.startsWith('row-audio') &&\n        r.actions.some(a => metaRef.current[a.id]?.asset === assetKey)\n      )\n      if (existingRow) {\n        newRows = rows.map(r =>\n          r.id === existingRow.id ? { ...r, actions: [...r.actions, action] } : r\n        )\n      } else {\n        // Create a new audio row\n        const audioRowCount = rows.filter(r => r.id.startsWith('row-audio')).length\n        const newRowId = `row-audio-${audioRowCount}`\n        const newRow: TimelineRow = { id: newRowId, actions: [action] }\n        // Insert before overlay row\n        const overlayIdx = rows.findIndex(r => r.id === 'row-overlay')\n        newRows = [...rows]\n        newRows.splice(overlayIdx >= 0 ? overlayIdx : rows.length, 0, newRow)\n      }\n    }\n\n    applyTimelineEdit(newRows, { [clipId]: clipMeta })\n  }, [data, rows, applyTimelineEdit])\n\n  const onTimelineDragOver = useCallback((e: React.DragEvent) => {\n    if (e.dataTransfer.types.includes('asset-key')) {\n      e.preventDefault()\n      e.currentTarget.classList.add('drag-over')\n    }\n  }, [])\n\n  const onTimelineDragLeave = useCallback((e: React.DragEvent) => {\n    e.currentTarget.classList.remove('drag-over')\n  }, [])\n\n  const onTimelineDrop = useCallback((e: React.DragEvent) => {\n    e.preventDefault()\n    e.currentTarget.classList.remove('drag-over')\n    const [REDACTED]('asset-key')\n    const trackType = e.dataTransfer.getData('track-type')\n    if (!assetKey) return\n\n    // Calculate time from drop X position\n    const rect = e.currentTarget.getBoundingClientRect()\n    const scrollLeft = e.currentTarget.scrollLeft\n    const startLeft = 20\n    const pixelsPerSecond = 160 / 5  // scaleWidth / scale\n    const dropX = e.clientX - rect.left\n    const time = Math.max(0, (dropX + scrollLeft - startLeft) / pixelsPerSecond)\n\n    handleAssetDrop(assetKey, trackType, time)\n  }, [handleAssetDrop])\n\n  // ── Rendering ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────"
}

> AGENT

All clean. Here's a summary of what was implemented:

**`viewer/src/timeline-data.ts`** — Added `inferTrackType(filePath)` and `getNextClipId(meta)` utility functions.

**`viewer/src/AssetPanel.tsx`** (new) — Draggable asset list component that:
- Shows unused assets by default, with a toggle to show all
- Excludes the background asset
- Each item is draggable with asset key + track type in `dataTransfer`
- Shows asset name, color-coded left border, and type badge (VIDEO/AUDIO/IMAGE)

**`viewer/src/App.tsx`** — 
- Wrapped `LivePreview` + `OverlayEditor` in `.preview-section`, added `AssetPanel` beside it
- Fixed `applyTimelineEdit` to support creating new meta entries (not just patching existing ones)
- Added `handleAssetDrop` — creates clips with sensible defaults per track type, finds/creates the right row
- Added `onDragOver`/`onDragLeave`/`onDrop` handlers on `.timeline-wrapper` that calculate drop time from X position

**`viewer/src/App.css`** — Added `.preview-section` flex wrapper, full asset panel styling, and `.drag-over` outline indicator on the timeline.

    12→export default function AssetPanel({ assetMap, rows, meta, backgroundAsset }: AssetPanelProps) {
    13→  const [showAll, setShowAll] = useState(false)
    14→
    15→  const usedAssets = useMemo(() => {
    16→    const used = new Set<string>()
    17→    for (const row of rows) {
    18→      for (const action of row.actions) {
    19→        const m = meta[action.id]
    20→        if (m) used.add(m.asset)

    22→    }
    23→    return used
    24→  }, [rows, meta])

    26→  const assets = useMemo(() => {

    30→      .map(([key, path]) => ({
    31→        key,
    32→        path,
    33→        trackType: inferTrackType(path),
    34→      }))
    35→  }, [assetMap, backgroundAsset, showAll, usedAssets])
    36→
    37→  const typeLabel = (t: string) => {

    46→    e.dataTransfer.setData('asset-key', key)
    47→    e.dataTransfer.setData('track-type', trackType)
    48→    e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = 'copy'

    50→

    63→        {assets.length === 0 && (

  "file_path": "/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/announcement-video/viewer/src/AssetPanel.tsx",
  "content": "import { useState, useMemo, useRef, useEffect } from 'react'\nimport type { TimelineRow } from '@xzdarcy/react-timeline-editor'\nimport { getAssetColor, inferTrackType, type ClipMeta } from './timeline-data'\n\ninterface AssetPanelProps {\n  assetMap: Record<string, string>\n  rows: TimelineRow[]\n  meta: Record<string, ClipMeta>\n  backgroundAsset?: string\n}\n\nexport default function AssetPanel({ assetMap, rows, meta, backgroundAsset }: AssetPanelProps) {\n  const [showAll, setShowAll] = useState(false)\n  const [hoveredAsset, setHoveredAsset] = useState<{ key: string; path: string; trackType: string } | null>(null)\n  const videoRef = useRef<HTMLVideoElement>(null)\n\n  const usedAssets = useMemo(() => {\n    const used = new Set<string>()\n    for (const row of rows) {\n      for (const action of row.actions) {\n        const m = meta[action.id]\n        if (m) used.add(m.asset)\n      }\n    }\n    return used\n  }, [rows, meta])\n\n  const assets = useMemo(() => {\n    return Object.entries(assetMap)\n      .filter(([key]) => key !== backgroundAsset)\n      .filter(([key]) => showAll || !usedAssets.has(key))\n      .map(([key, path]) => ({\n        key,\n        path,\n        trackType: inferTrackType(path),\n      }))\n  }, [assetMap, backgroundAsset, showAll, usedAssets])\n\n  // When hovered asset changes to a video, play it\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (hoveredAsset?.trackType === 'video' && videoRef.current) {\n      videoRef.current.currentTime = 0\n      videoRef.current.play().catch(() => {})\n    }\n  }, [hoveredAsset])\n\n  const typeLabel = (t: string) => {\n    if (t === 'video') return 'VIDEO'\n    if (t === 'audio') return 'AUDIO'\n    return 'IMAGE'\n  }\n\n  const typeBadgeClass = (t: string) => `asset-type-badge asset-type-${t}`\n\n  const onDragStart = (e: React.DragEvent, key: string, trackType: string) => {\n    e.dataTransfer.setData('asset-key', key)\n    e.dataTransfer.setData('track-type', trackType)\n    e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = 'copy'\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"asset-panel\">\n      <div className=\"asset-panel-header\">\n        <span className=\"asset-panel-title\">Assets</span>\n        <button\n          className=\"asset-toggle-btn\"\n          onClick={() => setShowAll(!showAll)}\n        >\n          {showAll ? 'Unused' : 'All'}\n        </button>\n      </div>\n      <div className=\"asset-list\">\n        {assets.length === 0 && (\n          <div className=\"asset-empty\">No unused assets</div>\n        )}\n        {assets.map(({ key, path, trackType }) => (\n          <div\n            key={key}\n            className={`asset-item${hoveredAsset?.key === key ? ' asset-item-active' : ''}`}\n            draggable\n            onDragStart={(e) => onDragStart(e, key, trackType)}\n            onMouseEnter={() => setHoveredAsset({ key, path, trackType })}\n            onMouseLeave={() => setHoveredAsset(null)}\n            style={{ borderLeftColor: getAssetColor(key) }}\n          >\n            <span className=\"asset-name\">{key}</span>\n            <span className={typeBadgeClass(trackType)}>{typeLabel(trackType)}</span>\n          </div>\n        ))}\n      </div>\n      {hoveredAsset && (\n        <div className=\"asset-preview\">\n          {hoveredAsset.trackType === 'video' && (\n            <video\n              ref={videoRef}\n              src={`/media/${hoveredAsset.path}`}\n              className=\"asset-preview-media\"\n              muted\n              loop\n              playsInline\n            />\n          )}\n          {hoveredAsset.trackType === 'overlay' && (\n            <img\n              src={`/media/${hoveredAsset.path}`}\n              className=\"asset-preview-media\"\n              alt={hoveredAsset.key}\n            />\n          )}\n          {hoveredAsset.trackType === 'audio' && (\n            <div className=\"asset-preview-audio\">\n              <span className=\"asset-preview-audio-icon\">♫</span>\n              <span className=\"asset-preview-audio-name\">{hoveredAsset.key}</span>\n            </div>\n          )}\n          <div className=\"asset-preview-label\">{hoveredAsset.path}</div>\n        </div>\n      )}\n    </div>\n  )\n}\n"
}
```

  "new_string": "  // ── Delete clip ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n  const handleDeleteClip = useCallback((actionId: string) => {\n    const newRows = rows.map(r => ({\n      ...r,\n      actions: r.actions.filter(a => a.id !== actionId),\n    }))\n    // Remove meta entry\n    setMeta(prev => {\n      const next = { ...prev }\n      delete next[actionId]\n      return next\n    })\n    applyTimelineEdit(newRows)\n  }, [rows, applyTimelineEdit])\n\n  // ── Rendering ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n  const getActionRender = useCallback((action: TimelineAction, _row: TimelineRow) => {\n    const m = meta[action.id]\n    if (!m) return null\n    const color = getAssetColor(m.asset)\n    const srcEnd = getSourceTime({ from: m.from, start: action.start, speed: m.speed }, action.end)\n    return (\n      <div\n        className=\"clip-action\"\n        style={{ backgroundColor: color }}\n      >\n        <span className=\"clip-label\">{m.asset}</span>\n        <span className=\"clip-time\">\n          src {m.from.toFixed(1)}s - {srcEnd.toFixed(1)}s\n        </span>\n        <button\n          className=\"clip-delete-btn\"\n          title=\"Delete clip\"\n          onMouseDown={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}\n          onClick={(e) => {\n            e.stopPropagation()\n            handleDeleteClip(action.id)\n          }}\n        >\n          ×\n        </button>\n      </div>\n    )\n  }, [meta, handleDeleteClip])"

  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/announcement-video/viewer/src/App.tsx",
  "old_string": "  // Keyboard: space to toggle play",
  "new_string": "  // Compute how many scale marks we need so the timeline is scrollable\n  const scaleCount = useMemo(() => {\n    let maxEnd = 0\n    for (const row of rows) {\n      for (const action of row.actions) {\n        if (action.end > maxEnd) maxEnd = action.end\n      }\n    }\n    // Add some padding (20s extra) and convert to scale units\n    return Math.ceil((maxEnd + 20) / scale) + 1\n  }, [rows, scale])\n\n  const handleZoomIn = useCallback(() => {\n    setScaleWidth(prev => Math.min(prev * 1.4, 500))\n  }, [])\n\n  const handleZoomOut = useCallback(() => {\n    setScaleWidth(prev => Math.max(prev / 1.4, 40))\n  }, [])\n\n  // Keyboard: space to toggle play"
}

> AGENT

Two fixes:

1. **Scrolling** — Added `scaleCount` prop to the Timeline, computed from the max end time of all clips + 20s padding. This tells the library to render enough scale marks for the full duration, making it scrollable.

2. **Zoom** — Added `−` / `+` buttons in the top bar that adjust `scaleWidth` (pixels per scale unit). Zooming in makes clips wider, zooming out compresses them. The drop handler also uses the dynamic values so drag-drop positions stay accurate at any zoom level.

> AGENT

There's the problem. The library's edit area and its internal virtualized grid have their `overflow` forced to `visible !important` — this kills the library's own internal scrolling. The library uses ReactVirtualized which manages its own scroll. The wrapper's `overflow-x: auto` can't help because the inner content has `overflow: visible` overriding the library's scroll behavior.

> TOOL

  "replace_all": false,

  "old_string": ".timeline-editor-edit-area {\n  overflow: visible !important;\n}\n\n.timeline-editor-edit-area .ReactVirtualized__Grid {\n  overflow: visible !important;\n}",
  "new_string": ".timeline-editor-edit-area {\n  overflow: auto !important;\n}\n\n.timeline-editor-edit-area .ReactVirtualized__Grid {\n  overflow: auto !important;\n}"
}

    22→function getRowLabel(rowId: string, meta: Record<string, ClipMeta>, rows: TimelineRow[]): string {
    23→  if (rowId === 'row-video') return 'Video'
    24→  if (rowId === 'row-overlay') return 'Overlay'
    25→  if (rowId.startsWith('row-audio')) {
    26→    // Find the asset name for this audio row
    27→    const row = rows.find(r => r.id === rowId)
    28→    if (row && row.actions.length > 0) {
    29→      const m = meta[row.actions[0].id]
    30→      if (m) return m.asset
    31→    }
    32→    return 'Audio'
    33→  }
    34→  return rowId
    35→}

    48→  const [meta, setMeta] = useState<Record<string, ClipMeta>>({})
    49→  const [transcript, setTranscript] = useState<TranscriptSegment[]>([])
    50→  const [saveStatus, setSaveStatus] = useState<'saved' | 'saving' | 'dirty' | 'error'>('saved')
    51→  const [renderStatus, setRenderStatus] = useState<'idle' | 'rendering' | 'done' | 'error'>('idle')
    52→  const [renderLog, setRenderLog] = useState('')
    53→  const [renderDirty, setRenderDirty] = useState(false)
    54→  const [scale, setScale] = useState(5)
    55→  const [scaleWidth, setScaleWidth] = useState(160)
    56→  const saveTimer = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null)
    57→  const headerRef = useRef<TimelineData['header'] | null>(null)
    58→
    59→  // Ref always holds the latest meta, so save callbacks don't need it as a dep
    60→  const metaRef = useRef(meta)
    61→  metaRef.current = meta
    62→
    63→  // Suppress onChange saves during poll-triggered state updates.
    64→  const ignoreNextOnChange = useRef(false)
    65→  useEffect(() => {
    66→    ignoreNextOnChange.current = false

    68→
    69→  // ── Single update path for all timeline edits ─────────────────────────────
    70→  // Every edit (drag, resize, poll) flows through here.
    71→
    72→  const saveToYaml = useCallback(async (currentRows: TimelineRow[]) => {
    73→    if (!headerRef.current) return
    74→    setSaveStatus('saving')
    75→    try {
    76→      const yamlStr = toYaml(currentRows, metaRef.current, headerRef.current)
    77→      lastYaml.current = yamlStr
    78→      const res = await fetch('/api/save-timeline', {

    83→      if (res.ok) {
    84→        setSaveStatus('saved')
    85→        setRenderDirty(true)
    86→      } else {
    87→        setSaveStatus('error')
    88→      }
    89→    } catch {
    90→      setSaveStatus('error')
    91→    }
    92→  }, [])
    93→
    94→  const scheduleSave = useCallback((currentRows: TimelineRow[]) => {
    95→    setSaveStatus('dirty')
    96→    if (saveTimer.current) clearTimeout(saveTimer.current)
    97→    saveTimer.current = setTimeout(() => saveToYaml(currentRows), 500)
    98→  }, [saveToYaml])
    99→
   100→  /**
   101→   * Central update function. All edits to rows/meta go through here.
   102→   * Ensures state is set, save is scheduled, and preview is refreshed.
   103→   */
   104→  const applyTimelineEdit = useCallback((
   105→    newRows: TimelineRow[],
   106→    metaUpdates?: Record<string, Partial<ClipMeta>>,
   107→  ) => {
   108→    setRows(newRows)
   109→
   110→    if (metaUpdates && Object.keys(metaUpdates).length > 0) {
   111→      setMeta(prev => {
   112→        const next = { ...prev }
   113→        for (const [id, patch] of Object.entries(metaUpdates)) {
   114→          next[id] = next[id] ? { ...next[id], ...patch } : patch as ClipMeta
   115→        }
   116→        return next
   117→      })
   118→    }
   119→
   120→    scheduleSave(newRows)
   121→    // Refresh preview after React commits the new state
   122→    queueMicrotask(() => previewRef.current?.refresh())
   123→  }, [scheduleSave])
   124→
   125→  // Keep canvasRef in sync with the preview's canvas element
   126→  useEffect(() => {

   131→
   132→  const lastYaml = useRef('')
   133→  useEffect(() => {

   135→      try {
   136→        const res = await fetch('/api/timeline')
   137→        const text = await res.text()
   138→        if (text !== lastYaml.current) {
   139→          lastYaml.current = text
   140→          const d = parseTimeline(text)
   141→          ignoreNextOnChange.current = true

   165→  }, [data])

   194→

   196→
   197→  // Track start positions before resize begins
   198→  const resizeStartRef = useRef<Record<string, { start: number; end: number; from: number }>>({})
   199→
   200→  const onActionResizeStart = useCallback(({ action }: { action: TimelineAction; row: TimelineRow; dir: string }) => {
   201→    const m = metaRef.current[action.id]

   203→    resizeStartRef.current[action.id] = { start: action.start, end: action.end, from: m.from }
   204→  }, [])
   205→
   206→  const onActionResizeEnd = useCallback(({ action, row, dir }: { action: TimelineAction; row: TimelineRow; dir: string; start: number; end: number }) => {

   213→        const startDelta = action.start - orig.start
   214→        metaUpdates[action.id] = {
   215→          from: Math.max(0, orig.from + startDelta * m.speed),
   216→        }
   217→      }
   218→    } else if (dir === 'right' && m) {
   219→      metaUpdates[action.id] = {
   220→        to: getSourceTime({ from: m.from, start: action.start, speed: m.speed }, action.end),
   221→      }
   222→    }
   223→
   224→    // Build updated rows with the final action positions
   225→    const newRows = rows.map(r => {
   226→      if (r.id !== row.id) return r
   227→      return {
   228→        ...r,
   229→        actions: r.actions.map(a =>
   230→          a.id === action.id ? { ...a, start: action.start, end: action.end } : a
   231→        ),
   232→      }
   233→    })
   234→
   235→    applyTimelineEdit(newRows, metaUpdates)
   236→    delete resizeStartRef.current[action.id]
   237→  }, [rows, applyTimelineEdit])
   238→
   239→  // Handle data changes from drag/move
   240→  const onChange = useCallback((newRows: TimelineRow[]) => {

   244→
   245→  // ── Overlay editing ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────

   247→  const onOverlayChange = useCallback((actionId: string, patch: Partial<ClipMeta>) => {

   250→
   251→  // ── Asset drag-drop ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   252→
   253→  const handleAssetDrop = useCallback((assetKey: string, trackType: string, time: number) => {
   254→    if (!data) return
   255→    const clipId = getNextClipId(metaRef.current)
   256→
   257→    let clipMeta: ClipMeta
   258→    let duration: number
   259→    if (trackType === 'video') {
   260→      duration = 5
   261→      clipMeta = { asset: assetKey, track: 'video', from: 0, to: 5, speed: 1, volume: 1, x: 0, y: 0, width: 1280, height: 720, opacity: 1 }
   262→    } else if (trackType === 'audio') {
   263→      duration = 10
   264→      clipMeta = { asset: assetKey, track: 'audio', from: 0, to: 10, speed: 1, volume: 1, x: 0, y: 0, width: 0, height: 0, opacity: 1 }
   265→    } else {
   266→      duration = 5
   267→      clipMeta = { asset: assetKey, track: 'overlay', from: 0, to: 0, speed: 1, volume: 1, x: 100, y: 100, width: 320, height: 240, opacity: 1 }
   268→    }
   269→
   270→    const effectId = `effect-${assetKey}`
   271→    if (data.effects && !data.effects[effectId]) {
   272→      data.effects[effectId] = { id: effectId }
   273→    }
   274→
   275→    const action: TimelineAction = {
   276→      id: clipId,
   277→      start: time,
   278→      end: time + duration,
   279→      effectId,
   280→    }
   281→
   282→    let newRows: TimelineRow[]
   283→    if (trackType === 'video') {
   284→      newRows = rows.map(r =>
   285→        r.id === 'row-video' ? { ...r, actions: [...r.actions, action] } : r
   286→      )
   287→    } else if (trackType === 'overlay') {
   288→      newRows = rows.map(r =>
   289→        r.id === 'row-overlay' ? { ...r, actions: [...r.actions, action] } : r
   290→      )
   291→    } else {
   292→      // Audio: find existing row for this asset or create new one
   293→      const existingRow = rows.find(r =>
   294→        r.id.startsWith('row-audio') &&
   295→        r.actions.some((a: TimelineAction) => metaRef.current[a.id]?.asset === assetKey)
   296→      )
   297→      if (existingRow) {
   298→        newRows = rows.map(r =>
   299→          r.id === existingRow.id ? { ...r, actions: [...r.actions, action] } : r
   300→        )
   301→      } else {
   302→        // Create a new audio row
   303→        const audioRowCount = rows.filter(r => r.id.startsWith('row-audio')).length
   304→        const newRowId = `row-audio-${audioRowCount}`
   305→        const newRow: TimelineRow = { id: newRowId, actions: [action] }
   306→        // Insert before overlay row
   307→        const overlayIdx = rows.findIndex(r => r.id === 'row-overlay')
   308→        newRows = [...rows]
   309→        newRows.splice(overlayIdx >= 0 ? overlayIdx : rows.length, 0, newRow)
   310→      }
   311→    }
   312→
   313→    applyTimelineEdit(newRows, { [clipId]: clipMeta })
   314→  }, [data, rows, applyTimelineEdit])
   315→
   316→  const onTimelineDragOver = useCallback((e: React.DragEvent) => {

   342→    handleAssetDrop(assetKey, trackType, time)

   391→
   392→  // Compute how many scale marks we need so the timeline is scrollable

   423→
   424→  const startRender = useCallback(async () => {
   425→    if (renderStatus === 'rendering') return
   426→    setRenderStatus('rendering')
   427→    setRenderLog('')
   428→    try {
   429→      const res = await fetch('/api/render', { method: 'POST' })
   430→      const reader = res.body?.getReader()
   431→      if (!reader) { setRenderStatus('error'); return }
   432→      const decoder = new TextDecoder()
   433→      let buf = ''
   434→      while (true) {
   435→        const { done, value } = await reader.read()
   436→        if (done) break
   437→        buf += decoder.decode(value, { stream: true })
   438→        const lines = buf.split('\n')
   439→        buf = lines.pop() ?? ''
   440→        for (const line of lines) {
   441→          if (line.startsWith('event: done')) {
   442→            setRenderStatus('done')
   443→            setRenderDirty(false)
   444→          } else if (line.startsWith('event: error')) {
   445→            setRenderStatus('error')

   490→            <button className="zoom-btn" onClick={handleZoomIn} title="Zoom in">+</button>
   491→          </div>
   492→          <span className="save-status" style={{ color: statusColor }}>

   494→          </span>

   498→

> AGENT

It's reasonably well-organized — the section comments help navigate the file. A few concrete issues though:

1. **`handleDeleteClip` bypasses `applyTimelineEdit` for meta** — it calls `setMeta` directly to remove the entry, then calls `applyTimelineEdit(newRows)` without meta updates. The save uses `metaRef.current` after a 500ms delay so it *probably* works, but it's inconsistent with the "all edits go through `applyTimelineEdit`" pattern.

2. **`data.effects` is mutated directly** on line 271-273 in `handleAssetDrop` — this mutates React state in place, which is a bug waiting to happen.

3. **`scale` is stateful but never changes** — `setScale` is unused; only `scaleWidth` drives zoom. `scale` could just be a constant.

Want me to fix these?

All three fixed:

1. **Delete now goes through `applyTimelineEdit`** — added a `metaDeletes` parameter so deletes use the same central path as everything else.
2. **Effects are updated immutably** via `setData` instead of mutating `data.effects` in place.
3. **`scale` is now a plain constant** instead of unused state.

    86→        const m = meta[action.id]
    87→        if (!m) continue
    88→        result.push({
    89→          actionId: action.id,
    90→          start: action.start,
    91→          end: action.end,
    92→          asset: m.asset,
    93→          from: m.from,
    94→          speed: m.speed,
    95→          volume: m.volume,
    96→          track: m.track,
    97→        })
    98→      }
    99→    }
   100→    return result
   101→  }, [rows, meta])
   102→
   103→  // Create and preload media elements
   104→  useEffect(() => {
   105→    const seenVideo = new Set<string>()
   106→    const seenAudio = new Set<string>()
   107→    const seenOverlay = new Set<string>()
   108→
   109→    for (const clip of clips) {
   110→      const path = assetPaths[clip.asset]
   111→      if (!path) continue
   112→      const url = `/media/${path}`
   113→
   114→      if (clip.track === 'video' && !seenVideo.has(clip.asset)) {
   115→        seenVideo.add(clip.asset)
   116→        if (!videoElements.current[clip.asset]) {
   117→          const vid = document.createElement('video')
   118→          vid.src = url
   119→          vid.preload = 'auto'
   120→          vid.muted = true
   121→          vid.playsInline = true
   122→          videoElements.current[clip.asset] = vid
   123→        }
   124→      }
   125→
   126→      if (clip.track === 'audio' && !seenAudio.has(clip.asset)) {
   127→        seenAudio.add(clip.asset)
   128→        if (!audioElements.current[clip.asset]) {
   129→          const aud = document.createElement('audio')
   130→          aud.src = url
   131→          aud.preload = 'auto'
   132→          audioElements.current[clip.asset] = aud
   133→        }
   134→      }
   135→
   136→      if (clip.track === 'overlay' && !seenOverlay.has(clip.asset)) {
   137→        seenOverlay.add(clip.asset)
   138→        if (!overlayImages.current[clip.asset]) {
   139→          const img = new Image()
   140→          img.src = url
   141→          overlayImages.current[clip.asset] = img
   142→        }
   143→      }
   144→    }
   145→
   146→    return () => {
   147→      for (const vid of Object.values(videoElements.current)) {
   148→        vid.pause()
   149→      }
   150→      for (const aud of Object.values(audioElements.current)) {
   151→        aud.pause()
   152→      }
   153→    }
   154→  }, [clips, assetPaths])
   155→
   156→  const findClipAt = useCallback(
   157→    (time: number, track: 'video' | 'audio'): ResolvedClip | null => {
   158→      return (
   159→        clips.find(
   160→          (c) => c.track === track && time >= c.start && time < c.end,
   161→        ) ?? null
   162→      )
   163→    },
   164→    [clips],
   165→  )
   166→
   167→  // ── Drawing functions ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   168→  // All draw functions target the offscreen buffer at full 1280x720.
   169→  // finishFrame() composites the buffer onto the visible canvas with background.
   170→
   171→  const getBuffer = useCallback(() => {
   172→    const buf = bufferCanvas.current
   173→    return { buf, ctx: buf.getContext('2d')! }
   174→  }, [])
   175→
   176→  const clearBuffer = useCallback(() => {
   177→    const { buf, ctx } = getBuffer()
   178→    if (backgroundScale < 1) {
   179→      // Transparent so background shows through
   180→      ctx.clearRect(0, 0, buf.width, buf.height)
   181→    } else {
   182→      ctx.fillStyle = '#000'
   183→      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, buf.width, buf.height)
   184→    }
   185→  }, [getBuffer, backgroundScale])
   186→
   187→  const drawBlack = useCallback(() => {
   188→    clearBuffer()
   189→  }, [clearBuffer])
   190→
   191→  // Draw transcript text (on buffer)
   192→  const drawTranscript = useCallback((time: number) => {
   193→    const { buf, ctx } = getBuffer()
   194→    clearBuffer()
   195→
   196→    // Find audio clip at this time to compute source time
   197→    const audioClip = findClipAt(time, 'audio')
   198→    if (!audioClip || transcript.length === 0) return
   199→
   200→    const sourceTime = getSourceTime(audioClip, time)
   201→
   202→    // Find matching transcript segment
   203→    const segment = transcript.find(s => sourceTime >= s.start && sourceTime < s.end)
   204→    if (!segment) return
   205→
   206→    const cw = buf.width
   207→    const ch = buf.height
   208→
   209→    ctx.fillStyle = '#cdd6f4'
   210→    ctx.font = '600 36px -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Segoe UI", Roboto, sans-serif'
   211→    ctx.textAlign = 'center'
   212→    ctx.textBaseline = 'middle'
   213→
   214→    // Word wrap
   215→    const maxWidth = cw * 0.75
   216→    const words = segment.text.split(' ')
   217→    const lines: string[] = []
   218→    let line = ''
   219→    for (const word of words) {
   220→      const test = line ? line + ' ' + word : word
   221→      if (ctx.measureText(test).width > maxWidth && line) {
   222→        lines.push(line)
   223→        line = word
   224→      } else {
   225→        line = test
   226→      }
   227→    }
   228→    if (line) lines.push(line)
   229→
   230→    const lineHeight = 48
   231→    const startY = ch / 2 - ((lines.length - 1) * lineHeight) / 2
   232→    for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
   233→      ctx.fillText(lines[i], cw / 2, startY + i * lineHeight)
   234→    }
   235→  }, [getBuffer, clearBuffer, findClipAt, transcript])
   236→
   237→  const drawVideoFrame = useCallback((vid: HTMLVideoElement) => {
   238→    const { buf, ctx } = getBuffer()
   239→
   240→    if (vid.readyState < 2 || !vid.videoWidth || !vid.videoHeight) {
   241→      clearBuffer()
   242→      return
   243→    }
   244→
   245→    const vw = vid.videoWidth
   246→    const vh = vid.videoHeight
   247→    const cw = buf.width
   248→    const ch = buf.height
   249→    const videoAspect = vw / vh
   250→    const canvasAspect = cw / ch
   251→
   252→    let dw: number, dh: number, dx: number, dy: number
   253→    if (videoAspect > canvasAspect) {
   254→      dw = cw
   255→      dh = cw / videoAspect
   256→      dx = 0
   257→      dy = (ch - dh) / 2
   258→    } else {
   259→      dh = ch
   260→      dw = ch * videoAspect
   261→      dx = (cw - dw) / 2
   262→      dy = 0
   263→    }
   264→
   265→    // Clear to transparent (or black if no background) so alpha is preserved
   266→    clearBuffer()
   267→    ctx.drawImage(vid, dx, dy, dw, dh)
   268→  }, [getBuffer, clearBuffer])
   269→
   270→  // Draw overlay images on top of the current frame (on buffer)
   271→  const drawOverlays = useCallback((time: number) => {
   272→    const { ctx } = getBuffer()
   273→
   274→    for (const clip of clips) {
   275→      if (clip.track !== 'overlay') continue
   276→      if (time < clip.start || time >= clip.end) continue
   277→
   278→      const m = meta[clip.actionId]
   279→      if (!m) continue
   280→
   281→      const img = overlayImages.current[clip.asset]
   282→      if (!img || !img.complete || img.naturalWidth <= 0) continue
   283→
   284→      ctx.save()
   285→      ctx.globalAlpha = m.opacity
   286→      ctx.drawImage(img, m.x, m.y, m.width, m.height)
   287→      ctx.restore()
   288→    }
   289→  }, [getBuffer, clips, meta])
   290→
   291→  // Composite buffer onto visible canvas: background + scaled content.
   292→  // This is the ONLY function that writes to the visible canvas.
   293→  const compositeToCanvas = useCallback(() => {
   294→    const canvas = canvasRef.current
   295→    if (!canvas) return
   296→    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d')
   297→    if (!ctx) return
   298→
   299→    const W = canvas.width
   300→    const H = canvas.height
   301→    const buf = bufferCanvas.current
   302→
   303→    const bg = bgImage.current
   304→    if (bg && bg.complete && bg.naturalWidth > 0 && backgroundScale < 1) {
   305→      // Draw background
   306→      ctx.drawImage(bg, 0, 0, W, H)
   307→      // Draw content scaled and centered
   308→      const cw = Math.round(W * backgroundScale)
   309→      const ch = Math.round(H * backgroundScale)
   310→      const cx = Math.round((W - cw) / 2)
   311→      const cy = Math.round((H - ch) / 2)
   312→      ctx.drawImage(buf, cx, cy, cw, ch)
   313→    } else {
   314→      // No background — copy buffer directly
   315→      ctx.drawImage(buf, 0, 0)
   316→    }
   317→  }, [backgroundScale])
   318→
   319→  // finishFrame: draw overlays on the buffer, then composite to visible canvas.
   320→  // Called once at the end of every frame render.
   321→  const finishFrame = useCallback((time: number) => {
   322→    drawOverlays(time)
   323→    compositeToCanvas()
   324→  }, [drawOverlays, compositeToCanvas])
   325→
   326→  // Find all audio clips active at a given time
   327→  const findAudioClipsAt = useCallback(
   328→    (time: number): ResolvedClip[] => {
   329→      return clips.filter(
   330→        (c) => c.track === 'audio' && time >= c.start && time < c.end,
   331→      )
   332→    },
   333→    [clips],
   334→  )
   335→
   336→  // Sync all audio tracks for a given timeline time
   337→  const syncAudio = useCallback(
   338→    (time: number, shouldPlay: boolean) => {
   339→      const activeClips = findAudioClipsAt(time)
   340→      const activeIds = new Set(activeClips.map(c => c.actionId))
   341→
   342→      // Pause audio elements that are no longer active
   343→      for (const prevId of activeAudioClips.current) {
   344→        if (!activeIds.has(prevId)) {
   345→          const prevClip = clips.find(c => c.actionId === prevId)
   346→          if (prevClip) {
   347→            audioElements.current[prevClip.asset]?.pause()
   348→          }
   349→        }
   350→      }
   351→
   352→      if (activeClips.length === 0) {
   353→        activeAudioClips.current = new Set()
   354→        return
   355→      }
   356→
   357→      for (const clip of activeClips) {
   358→        const aud = audioElements.current[clip.asset]
   359→        if (!aud) continue
   360→
   361→        const sourceTime = getSourceTime(clip, time)
   362→        aud.volume = clip.volume
   363→
   364→        if (shouldPlay) {
   365→          if (
   366→            !activeAudioClips.current.has(clip.actionId) ||
   367→            Math.abs(aud.currentTime - sourceTime) > 0.3
   368→          ) {
   369→            aud.currentTime = sourceTime
   370→          }
   371→          if (aud.paused) aud.play()
   372→        } else {
   373→          aud.currentTime = sourceTime
   374→          aud.pause()
   375→        }
   376→      }
   377→
   378→      activeAudioClips.current = activeIds
   379→    },
   380→    [findAudioClipsAt, clips],
   381→  )
   382→
   383→  // Draw a video frame once the element is ready, with fallback
   384→  const drawWhenReady = useCallback((vid: HTMLVideoElement, time: number) => {
   385→    // Try requestVideoFrameCallback (fires when a decoded frame is available)
   386→    if ('requestVideoFrameCallback' in vid) {
   387→      vid.requestVideoFrameCallback(() => {
   388→        drawVideoFrame(vid)
   389→        finishFrameRef.current(time)
   390→      })
   391→    }
   392→    // Also listen for seeked as backup
   393→    const onSeeked = () => {
   394→      drawVideoFrame(vid)
   395→      finishFrameRef.current(time)
   396→      vid.removeEventListener('seeked', onSeeked)
   397→    }
   398→    vid.addEventListener('seeked', onSeeked)
   399→    // Fallback timeout in case neither fires (e.g. already at target time)
   400→    setTimeout(() => {
   401→      vid.removeEventListener('seeked', onSeeked)
   402→      drawVideoFrame(vid)
   403→      finishFrameRef.current(time)
   404→    }, 100)
   405→  }, [drawVideoFrame])
   406→
   407→  // Sync video for a given timeline time
   408→  const syncVideo = useCallback(
   409→    (time: number, shouldPlay: boolean) => {
   410→      const clip = findClipAt(time, 'video')
   411→
   412→      if (!clip) {
   413→        // In a gap — pause any playing video, show transcript
   414→        if (activeVideoClip.current) {
   415→          const prevAsset = clips.find(
   416→            (c) => c.actionId === activeVideoClip.current,
   417→          )?.asset
   418→          if (prevAsset && videoElements.current[prevAsset]) {
   419→            videoElements.current[prevAsset].pause()
   420→          }
   421→          activeVideoClip.current = null
   422→        }
   423→        drawTranscript(time)
   424→        finishFrameRef.current(time)
   425→        return
   426→      }
   427→
   428→      const vid = videoElements.current[clip.asset]
   429→      if (!vid) {
   430→        drawTranscript(time)
   431→        finishFrameRef.current(time)
   432→        return
   433→      }
   434→
   435→      const sourceTime = getSourceTime(clip, time)
   436→
   437→      if (shouldPlay) {
   438→        // Switch clip or correct drift
   439→        if (
   440→          activeVideoClip.current !== clip.actionId ||
   441→          Math.abs(vid.currentTime - sourceTime) > 0.3
   442→        ) {
   443→          // Pause previous if different clip
   444→          if (activeVideoClip.current && activeVideoClip.current !== clip.actionId) {
   445→            const prevAsset = clips.find(
   446→              (c) => c.actionId === activeVideoClip.current,
   447→            )?.asset
   448→            if (prevAsset && videoElements.current[prevAsset]) {
   449→              videoElements.current[prevAsset].pause()
   450→            }
   451→          }
   452→          vid.currentTime = sourceTime
   453→          vid.playbackRate = clip.speed
   454→          activeVideoClip.current = clip.actionId
   455→          vid.play()
   456→        }
   457→        drawVideoFrame(vid)
   458→        finishFrameRef.current(time)
   459→      } else {
   460→        // Paused — seek and draw when frame is ready
   461→        vid.pause()
   462→        vid.currentTime = sourceTime
   463→        activeVideoClip.current = clip.actionId
   464→        drawWhenReady(vid, time)
   465→      }
   466→    },
   467→    [findClipAt, clips, drawVideoFrame, drawTranscript, drawWhenReady],
   468→  )
   469→
   470→  // Refs so the RAF loop always sees the latest sync functions.
   471→  const syncVideoRef = useRef(syncVideo)
   472→  const syncAudioRef = useRef(syncAudio)
   473→  const onTimeUpdateRef = useRef(onTimeUpdate)
   474→  const finishFrameRef = useRef(finishFrame)
   475→  syncVideoRef.current = syncVideo
   476→  syncAudioRef.current = syncAudio
   477→  onTimeUpdateRef.current = onTimeUpdate
   478→  finishFrameRef.current = finishFrame
   479→
   480→  // RAF playback loop
   481→  const tick = useCallback(() => {
   482→    if (!playing.current) return
   483→
   484→    const elapsed = (performance.now() - startWall.current) / 1000
   485→    const time = startTimeline.current + elapsed
   486→    currentTime.current = time
   487→
   488→    syncVideoRef.current(time, true)
   489→    syncAudioRef.current(time, true)
   490→    onTimeUpdateRef.current(time)
   491→
   492→    rafId.current = requestAnimationFrame(tick)
   493→  }, [])
   494→
   495→  const seek = useCallback(
   496→    (time: number) => {
   497→      currentTime.current = time
   498→      if (playing.current) {
   499→        startWall.current = performance.now()
   500→        startTimeline.current = time
   501→      }
   502→      syncVideo(time, playing.current)
   503→      syncAudio(time, playing.current)
   504→      onTimeUpdate(time)
   505→    },
   506→    [syncVideo, syncAudio, onTimeUpdate],
   507→  )
   508→
   509→  const play = useCallback(() => {
   510→    if (playing.current) return
   511→    playing.current = true
   512→    setIsPlaying(true)
   513→    startWall.current = performance.now()
   514→    startTimeline.current = currentTime.current
   515→    rafId.current = requestAnimationFrame(tick)
   516→  }, [tick])
   517→
   518→  const pause = useCallback(() => {
   519→    if (!playing.current) return
   520→    playing.current = false
   521→    setIsPlaying(false)
   522→    cancelAnimationFrame(rafId.current)
   523→    for (const vid of Object.values(videoElements.current)) {
   524→      vid.pause()
   525→    }
   526→    for (const aud of Object.values(audioElements.current)) {
   527→      aud.pause()
   528→    }
   529→    activeVideoClip.current = null
   530→    activeAudioClips.current = new Set()
   531→    syncVideo(currentTime.current, false)
   532→  }, [syncVideo])
   533→
   534→  const togglePlayPause = useCallback(() => {
   535→    if (playing.current) {
   536→      pause()
   537→    } else {
   538→      play()
   539→    }
   540→  }, [play, pause])
   541→
   542→  // Force re-sync using refs (always has latest data, no stale closures)
   543→  const refresh = useCallback(() => {
   544→    activeVideoClip.current = null
   545→    activeAudioClips.current = new Set()
   546→    const t = currentTime.current
   547→    if (playing.current) {
   548→      startWall.current = performance.now()
   549→      startTimeline.current = t
   550→    }
   551→    syncVideoRef.current(t, playing.current)
   552→    syncAudioRef.current(t, playing.current)
   553→  }, [])
   554→
   555→  useImperativeHandle(
   556→    ref,
   557→    () => ({
   558→      seek,
   559→      play,
   560→      pause,
   561→      togglePlayPause,
   562→      refresh,
   563→      get isPlaying() {
   564→        return playing.current
   565→      },
   566→      get canvasElement() {
   567→        return canvasRef.current
   568→      },
   569→    }),
   570→    [seek, play, pause, togglePlayPause, refresh],
   571→  )
   572→
   573→  // When clips change while paused, re-draw so the preview reflects
   574→  // the new positions. (During playback, the RAF loop handles this
   575→  // automatically via syncVideoRef/syncAudioRef.)
   576→  useEffect(() => {
   577→    if (!playing.current) {
   578→      activeVideoClip.current = null
   579→      activeAudioClips.current = new Set()
   580→      syncVideo(currentTime.current, false)
   581→      syncAudio(currentTime.current, false)
   582→    }
   583→  }, [clips, syncVideo, syncAudio])
   584→
   585→  // Draw initial black frame
   586→  useEffect(() => {
   587→    drawBlack()
   588→    compositeToCanvas()
   589→  }, [drawBlack, compositeToCanvas])
   590→
   591→  return (
   592→    <div className="live-preview">
   593→      <canvas
   594→        ref={canvasRef}
   595→        width={1280}
   596→        height={720}
   597→        className="preview-canvas"
   598→      />
   599→      <button className="skip-btn" onClick={() => seek(0)} title="Jump to beginning">
   600→        ⏮
   601→      </button>
   602→      <button className="play-btn" onClick={togglePlayPause}>
   603→        {isPlaying ? '⏸' : '▶'}
   604→      </button>
   605→    </div>
   606→  )
   607→})
   608→
   609→export default LivePreview
   610→
```
