1→import { defineConfig } from 'vite'
     2→import type { Plugin } from 'vite'
     3→import react from '@vitejs/plugin-react'
     4→import path from 'path'
     5→import fs from 'fs'
     6→import { spawn } from 'child_process'
     7→
     8→const projectRoot = path.resolve(__dirname, '..')
     9→const timelineFile = path.join(projectRoot, 'timeline.yaml')
    10→
    11→function timelineApiPlugin(): Plugin {
    12→  let lastSaveFromBrowser = 0
    13→
    14→  return {
    15→    name: 'timeline-api',
    16→    configureServer(server) {
    17→      // Watch timeline.yaml with polling (fs.watch is flaky on macOS)
    18→      let lastMtime = 0
    19→      try { lastMtime = fs.statSync(timelineFile).mtimeMs } catch {}
    20→      setInterval(() => {
    21→        try {
    22→          const mtime = fs.statSync(timelineFile).mtimeMs
    23→          if (mtime > lastMtime) {
    24→            lastMtime = mtime
    25→            // Skip if the change came from the browser's own save (within 2s)
    26→            if (Date.now() - lastSaveFromBrowser < 2000) return
    27→            server.ws.send({ type: 'custom', event: 'timeline-changed' })
    28→          }
    29→        } catch {}
    30→      }, 500)
    31→
    32→      // Read timeline.yaml (no caching)
    33→      server.middlewares.use('/api/timeline', (req, res) => {
    34→        if (req.method !== 'GET') { res.statusCode = 405; res.end(); return }
    35→        try {
    36→          const content = fs.readFileSync(timelineFile, 'utf-8')
    37→          res.setHeader('Content-Type', 'text/yaml')
    38→          res.setHeader('Cache-Control', 'no-store')
    39→          res.end(content)
    40→        } catch (err) {
    41→          res.statusCode = 500
    42→          res.end(String(err))
    43→        }
    44→      })
    45→
    46→      // Save timeline.yaml
    47→      server.middlewares.use('/api/save-timeline', (req, res) => {
    48→        if (req.method !== 'POST') {
    49→          res.statusCode = 405
    50→          res.end('Method not allowed')
    51→          return
    52→        }
    53→        let body = ''
    54→        req.on('data', (chunk: Buffer) => { body += chunk.toString() })
    55→        req.on('end', () => {
    56→          try {
    57→            lastSaveFromBrowser = Date.now()
    58→            fs.writeFileSync(timelineFile, body, 'utf-8')
    59→            res.setHeader('Content-Type', 'application/json')
    60→            res.end(JSON.stringify({ ok: true }))
    61→          } catch (err) {
    62→            res.statusCode = 500
    63→            res.end(JSON.stringify({ error: String(err) }))
    64→          }
    65→        })
    66→      })
    67→
    68→      // Waveform data for audio files (cached)
    69→      server.middlewares.use('/api/waveform', (req, res) => {
    70→        if (req.method !== 'GET') { res.statusCode = 405; res.end(); return }
    71→        const asset = (req.url?.split('?asset=')[1] || '').split('&')[0]
    72→        if (!asset) { res.statusCode = 400; res.end('asset required'); return }
    73→
    74→        // Look up asset path from asset_profiles
    75→        const profilePath = path.join(projectRoot, 'asset_profiles', `${asset}.yaml`)
    76→        if (!fs.existsSync(profilePath)) { res.statusCode = 404; res.end('not found'); return }
    77→
    78→        try {
    79→          const yaml = require('js-yaml')
    80→          const profile = yaml.load(fs.readFileSync(profilePath, 'utf-8')) as any
    81→
    82→          if (profile.silence_regions) {
    83→            res.setHeader('Content-Type', 'application/json')
    84→            res.end(JSON.stringify({
    85→              asset,
    86→              duration: profile.duration_s,
    87→              silence: profile.silence_regions,
    88→              transcript: profile.transcript?.words || [],
    89→            }))
    90→          } else {
    91→            res.statusCode = 400
    92→            res.end('not an audio asset')
    93→          }
    94→        } catch (err) {
    95→          res.statusCode = 500
    96→          res.end(String(err))
    97→        }
    98→      })
    99→
   100→      // Render preview
   101→      server.middlewares.use('/api/render', (req, res) => {
   102→        if (req.method !== 'POST') {
   103→          res.statusCode = 405
   104→          res.end('Method not allowed')
   105→          return
   106→        }
   107→
   108→        res.setHeader('Content-Type', 'text/event-stream')
   109→        res.setHeader('Cache-Control', 'no-cache')
   110→        res.setHeader('Connection', 'keep-alive')
   111→
   112→        const send = (event: string, data: string) => {
   113→          res.write(`event: ${event}\ndata: ${data}\n\n`)
   114→        }
   115→
   116→        send('status', 'Rendering preview...')
   117→
   118→        const proc = spawn('python3', ['tools/render.py', '--preview'], {
   119→          cwd: projectRoot,
   120→          stdio: ['ignore', 'pipe', 'pipe'],
   121→        })
   122→
   123→        proc.stdout.on('data', (chunk: Buffer) => {
   124→          send('log', chunk.toString().trim())
   125→        })
   126→
   127→        proc.stderr.on('data', (chunk: Buffer) => {
   128→          send('log', chunk.toString().trim())
   129→        })
   130→
   131→        proc.on('close', (code) => {
   132→          if (code === 0) {
   133→            send('done', 'Render complete')
   134→          } else {
   135→            send('error', `Render failed (exit code ${code})`)
   136→          }
   137→          res.end()
   138→        })
   139→
   140→        proc.on('error', (err) => {
   141→          send('error', String(err))
   142→          res.end()
   143→        })
   144→      })
   145→    },
   146→  }
   147→}
   148→
   149→export default defineConfig({
   150→  plugins: [react(), timelineApiPlugin()],
   151→  resolve: {
   152→    alias: {
   153→      '@project': projectRoot,
   154→    },
   155→  },
   156→  server: {
   157→    fs: {
   158→      allow: [projectRoot],
   159→    },
   160→    proxy: {
   161→      '/media': {
   162→        target: 'http://localhost:5173',
   163→        rewrite: (p) => '/@fs' + path.join(projectRoot, p.replace(/^\/media/, '')),
   164→      },
   165→    },
   166→  },
   167→})
   168→

1→import { useState, useMemo, useRef, useEffect } from 'react'
     2→import type { TimelineRow } from '@xzdarcy/react-timeline-editor'
     3→import { getAssetColor, inferTrackType, type ClipMeta } from './timeline-data'
     4→
     5→interface AssetPanelProps {
     6→  assetMap: Record<string, string>
     7→  rows: TimelineRow[]
     8→  meta: Record<string, ClipMeta>
     9→  backgroundAsset?: string
    10→}
    11→
    12→export default function AssetPanel({ assetMap, rows, meta, backgroundAsset }: AssetPanelProps) {
    13→  const [showAll, setShowAll] = useState(false)
    14→  const [hoveredAsset, setHoveredAsset] = useState<{ key: string; path: string; trackType: string } | null>(null)
    15→  const videoRef = useRef<HTMLVideoElement>(null)
    16→
    17→  const usedAssets = useMemo(() => {
    18→    const used = new Set<string>()
    19→    for (const row of rows) {
    20→      for (const action of row.actions) {
    21→        const m = meta[action.id]
    22→        if (m) used.add(m.asset)
    23→      }
    24→    }
    25→    return used
    26→  }, [rows, meta])
    27→
    28→  const assets = useMemo(() => {
    29→    return Object.entries(assetMap)
    30→      .filter(([key]) => key !== backgroundAsset)
    31→      .filter(([key]) => showAll || !usedAssets.has(key))
    32→      .map(([key, path]) => ({
    33→        key,
    34→        path,
    35→        trackType: inferTrackType(path),
    36→      }))
    37→  }, [assetMap, backgroundAsset, showAll, usedAssets])
    38→
    39→  // When hovered asset changes to a video, play it
    40→  useEffect(() => {
    41→    if (hoveredAsset?.trackType === 'video' && videoRef.current) {
    42→      videoRef.current.currentTime = 0
    43→      videoRef.current.play().catch(() => {})
    44→    }
    45→  }, [hoveredAsset])
    46→
    47→  const typeLabel = (t: string) => {
    48→    if (t === 'video') return 'VIDEO'
    49→    if (t === 'audio') return 'AUDIO'
    50→    return 'IMAGE'
    51→  }
    52→
    53→  const typeBadgeClass = (t: string) => `asset-type-badge asset-type-${t}`
    54→
    55→  const onDragStart = (e: React.DragEvent, key: string, trackType: string) => {
    56→    e.dataTransfer.setData('asset-key', key)
    57→    e.dataTransfer.setData('track-type', trackType)
    58→    e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = 'copy'
    59→  }
    60→
    61→  return (
    62→    <div className="asset-panel">
    63→      <div className="asset-panel-header">
    64→        <span className="asset-panel-title">Assets</span>
    65→        <button
    66→          className="asset-toggle-btn"
    67→          onClick={() => setShowAll(!showAll)}
    68→        >
    69→          {showAll ? 'Unused' : 'All'}
    70→        </button>
    71→      </div>
    72→      <div className="asset-list">
    73→        {assets.length === 0 && (
    74→          <div className="asset-empty">No unused assets</div>
    75→        )}
    76→        {assets.map(({ key, path, trackType }) => (
    77→          <div
    78→            key={key}
    79→            className={`asset-item${hoveredAsset?.key === key ? ' asset-item-active' : ''}`}
    80→            draggable
    81→            onDragStart={(e) => onDragStart(e, key, trackType)}
    82→            onMouseEnter={() => setHoveredAsset({ key, path, trackType })}
    83→            onMouseLeave={() => setHoveredAsset(null)}
    84→            style={{ borderLeftColor: getAssetColor(key) }}
    85→          >
    86→            <span className="asset-name">{key}</span>
    87→            <span className={typeBadgeClass(trackType)}>{typeLabel(trackType)}</span>
    88→          </div>
    89→        ))}
    90→      </div>
    91→      {hoveredAsset && (
    92→        <div className="asset-preview">
    93→          {hoveredAsset.trackType === 'video' && (
    94→            <video
    95→              ref={videoRef}
    96→              src={`/media/${hoveredAsset.path}`}
    97→              className="asset-preview-media"
    98→              muted
    99→              loop
   100→              playsInline
   101→            />
   102→          )}
   103→          {hoveredAsset.trackType === 'overlay' && (
   104→            <img
   105→              src={`/media/${hoveredAsset.path}`}
   106→              className="asset-preview-media"
   107→              alt={hoveredAsset.key}
   108→            />
   109→          )}
   110→          {hoveredAsset.trackType === 'audio' && (
   111→            <div className="asset-preview-audio">
   112→              <span className="asset-preview-audio-icon">♫</span>
   113→              <span className="asset-preview-audio-name">{hoveredAsset.key}</span>
   114→            </div>
   115→          )}
   116→          <div className="asset-preview-label">{hoveredAsset.path}</div>
   117→        </div>
   118→      )}
   119→    </div>
   120→  )
   121→}

ClipMeta } from './timeline-data'\n\ninterface AssetPanelProps {\n  assetMap: Record<string, string>\n  rows: TimelineRow[]\n  meta: Record<string, ClipMeta>\n  backgroundAsset?: string\n}\n\nconst ACCEPTED_EXTENSIONS = [\n  '.mp4', '.webm', '.mov',\n  '.mp3', '.wav', '.aac', '.m4a',\n  '.jpg', '.jpeg', '.png', '.gif', '.svg',\n]\n\nfunction isMediaFile(file: File): boolean {\n  const ext = file.name.slice(file.name.lastIndexOf('.')).toLowerCase()\n  return ACCEPTED_EXTENSIONS.includes(ext)\n}\n\nexport default function AssetPanel({ assetMap, rows, meta, backgroundAsset }: AssetPanelProps) {\n  const [showAll, setShowAll] = useState(false)\n  const [hoveredAsset, setHoveredAsset] = useState<{ key: string; path: string; trackType: string } | null>(null)\n  const [fileDragOver, setFileDragOver] = useState(false)\n  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false)\n  const videoRef = useRef<HTMLVideoElement>(null)\n  const dragCounter = useRef(0)\n\n  const usedAssets = useMemo(() => {\n    const used = new Set<string>()\n    for (const row of rows) {\n      for (const action of row.actions) {\n        const m = meta[action.id]\n        if (m) used.add(m.asset)\n      }\n    }\n    return used\n  }, [rows, meta])\n\n  const assets = useMemo(() => {\n    return Object.entries(assetMap)\n      .filter(([key]) => key !== backgroundAsset)\n      .filter(([key]) => showAll || !usedAssets.has(key))\n      .map(([key, path]) => ({\n        key,\n        path,\n        trackType: inferTrackType(path),\n      }))\n  }, [assetMap, backgroundAsset, showAll, usedAssets])\n\n  // When hovered asset changes to a video, play it\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (hoveredAsset?.trackType === 'video' && videoRef.current) {\n      videoRef.current.currentTime = 0\n      videoRef.current.play().catch(() => {})\n    }\n  }, [hoveredAsset])\n\n  const typeLabel = (t: string) => {\n    if (t === 'video') return 'VIDEO'\n    if (t === 'audio') return 'AUDIO'\n    return 'IMAGE'\n  }\n\n  const typeBadgeClass = (t: string) => `asset-type-badge asset-type-${t}`\n\n  const onDragStart = (e: React.DragEvent, key: string, trackType: string) => {\n    e.dataTransfer.setData('asset-key', key)\n    e.dataTransfer.setData('track-type', trackType)\n    e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = 'copy'\n  }\n\n  // File drop handling\n  const hasFiles = (e: React.DragEvent) => e.dataTransfer.types.includes('Files')\n\n  const onFileDragEnter = useCallback((e: React.DragEvent) => {\n    if (!hasFiles(e)) return\n    e.preventDefault()\n    dragCounter.current++\n    setFileDragOver(true)\n  }, [])\n\n  const onFileDragOver = useCallback((e: React.DragEvent) => {\n    if (!hasFiles(e)) return\n    e.preventDefault()\n    e.dataTransfer.dropEffect = 'copy'\n  }, [])\n\n  const onFileDragLeave = useCallback((e: React.DragEvent) => {\n    if (!hasFiles(e)) return\n    e.preventDefault()\n    dragCounter.current--\n    if (dragCounter.current <= 0) {\n      dragCounter.current = 0\n      setFileDragOver(false)\n    }\n  }, [])\n\n  const uploadFile = useCallback(async (file: File) => {\n    const res = await fetch('/api/upload', {\n      method: 'POST',\n      headers: { 'X-Filename': encodeURIComponent(file.name) },\n      body: file,\n    })\n    if (!res.ok) {\n      const err = await res.json()\n      throw new Error(err.error || 'Upload failed')\n    }\n    return res.json()\n  }, [])\n\n  const onFileDrop = useCallback(async (e: React.DragEvent) => {\n    e.preventDefault()\n    dragCounter.current = 0\n    setFileDragOver(false)\n\n    const files = Array.from(e.dataTransfer.files).filter(isMediaFile)\n    if (files.length === 0) return\n\n    setUploading(true)\n    try {\n      await Promise.all(files.map(uploadFile))\n    } catch (err) {\n      console.error('Upload failed:', err)\n    } finally {\n      setUploading(false)\n    }\n  }, [uploadFile])\n\n  return (\n    <div\n      className=\"asset-panel\"\n      onDragEnter={onFileDragEnter}\n      onDragOver={onFileDragOver}\n      onDragLeave={onFileDragLeave}\n      onDrop={onFileDrop}\n    >\n      <div className=\"asset-panel-header\">\n        <span className=\"asset-panel-title\">Assets</span>\n        <button\n          className=\"asset-toggle-btn\"\n          onClick={() => setShowAll(!showAll)}\n        >\n          {showAll ? 'Unused' : 'All'}\n        </button>\n      </div>\n      <div className=\"asset-list\">\n        {assets.length === 0 && !fileDragOver && !uploading && (\n          <div className=\"asset-empty\">No unused assets</div>\n        )}\n        {assets.map(({ key, path, trackType }) => (\n          <div\n            key={key}\n            className={`asset-item${hoveredAsset?.key === key ? }

> AGENT

Done. Here's what happens now when you drag a media file from Finder onto the asset panel:

1. A dashed blue **drop zone overlay** appears with a "+" icon and "Drop to import" text
2. On drop, the file is uploaded to `inputs/` via a new `/api/upload` endpoint
3. The server derives an asset key from the filename and adds it to `asset_map` in `timeline.yaml`
4. The polling loop picks up the YAML change, so the new asset appears in the panel automatically
5. Supports multiple files at once, filters to accepted media extensions only

```
# Reigh Project Overview

## What It Is

Reigh is an **AI-powered image and video generation studio** built as a web application. Per the README: "AI-powered image and video generation studio. For usage, go to reigh.art — this repo is for development only."

The project serves as both a production platform and an open-source development repository, with the live service accessible separately from this codebase.

## Tech Stack

The application is constructed with:
- **Frontend**: React + Vite + TypeScript
- **Styling**: TailwindCSS with shadcn-ui components
- **Backend/Infrastructure**: Supabase (Postgres, Auth, Storage, Edge Functions)
- **Language Distribution**: 78.7% TypeScript, 9.7% PLpgSQL, 9.0% HTML, 1.4% CSS

## Architecture & Governance

The project emphasizes code quality and maintainability through:
- Formal governance contracts for key systems (Supabase runtime, error handling, UI components)
- Automated test gates: `test:contracts`, `test:arch`, and `quality:check`
- Comprehensive documentation including architecture overviews and 24 detailed sub-documents

## Visual/Branding Elements

The repository includes a **scorecard.png** image (displaying code quality metrics), but no traditional logo or visual branding assets are described in the README. The project maintains a professional, developer-focused presentation without prominent visual identity markers in the documentation.
```

# Reigh HTML Structure Analysis

## Meta Tags & Theme Configuration

**Theme Color:** `#b8860b` (goldenrod)

**Core Meta Tags:**
- Description: "Reigh is an open-source tool for making art. Create images and travel between them using powerful, controllable models - for free on your computer, or in the cloud."
- Author: Reigh
- Viewport: `width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0, viewport-fit=cover`

**PWA Configuration:**
- App title: "Reigh"
- Status bar style: default
- Apple touch icon: `/favicon-192x192.png`
- Manifest: `/manifest.json`

## Font & Style References

Three typeface families are preconnected via Google Fonts:
- Crimson Text (weights: 400, 600, 700; both regular and italic)
- Inter (weights: 400, 500, 700)
- Playfair Display (variable weight range 400–900)

## Technical Features

**Performance optimizations include:**
- Preconnect directives for fonts.googleapis.com and fonts.gstatic.com
- Direct stylesheet loading to prevent cumulative layout shift
- Dynamic preloading of critical images via custom hook

**Progressive Web App support:**
- Service worker registration script targeting `/sw.js`
- Manifest-based PWA configuration

**Entry point:** TypeScript/JSX module at `/src/app/main.tsx`

Here are all three:

All saved to `inputs/`.

Regarding your question — the uploaded files go into `inputs/` because that's where the project stores source media (per the project structure in CLAUDE.md). The asset panel upload endpoint saves there and auto-adds to `asset_map` in `timeline.yaml`, so they're immediately available. Did you mean you want them to go somewhere else?

> DEVELOPER

so why aren't they showing in the assets thing i mean?

> AGENT

Because the upload endpoint adds them to `asset_map` in `timeline.yaml`, but the `imagegen` tool doesn't — it just saves the file to `inputs/` and tells you to manually add it. The images are there but not in the timeline's `asset_map`, so the viewer doesn't know about them.

Want me to add them to the `asset_map` now?

1→import { defineConfig } from 'vite'
     2→import type { Plugin } from 'vite'
     3→import react from '@vitejs/plugin-react'

     7→import yaml from 'js-yaml'
     8→
     9→const projectRoot = path.resolve(__dirname, '..')
    10→const timelineFile = path.join(projectRoot, 'timeline.yaml')
    11→
    12→function timelineApiPlugin(): Plugin {
    13→  let lastSaveFromBrowser = 0
    14→
    15→  return {
    16→    name: 'timeline-api',
    17→    configureServer(server) {
    18→      // Watch timeline.yaml with polling (fs.watch is flaky on macOS)
    19→      let lastMtime = 0
    20→      try { lastMtime = fs.statSync(timelineFile).mtimeMs } catch {}
    21→      setInterval(() => {
    22→        try {
    23→          const mtime = fs.statSync(timelineFile).mtimeMs
    24→          if (mtime > lastMtime) {
    25→            lastMtime = mtime
    26→            // Skip if the change came from the browser's own save (within 2s)
    27→            if (Date.now() - lastSaveFromBrowser < 2000) return
    28→            server.ws.send({ type: 'custom', event: 'timeline-changed' })
    29→          }
    30→        } catch {}
    31→      }, 500)
    32→
    33→      // Read timeline.yaml (no caching)
    34→      server.middlewares.use('/api/timeline', (req, res) => {
    35→        if (req.method !== 'GET') { res.statusCode = 405; res.end(); return }
    36→        try {
    37→          const content = fs.readFileSync(timelineFile, 'utf-8')
    38→          res.setHeader('Content-Type', 'text/yaml')
    39→          res.setHeader('Cache-Control', 'no-store')
    40→          res.end(content)
    41→        } catch (err) {
    42→          res.statusCode = 500
    43→          res.end(String(err))
    44→        }
    45→      })
    46→
    47→      // Save timeline.yaml
    48→      server.middlewares.use('/api/save-timeline', (req, res) => {
    49→        if (req.method !== 'POST') {
    50→          res.statusCode = 405
    51→          res.end('Method not allowed')
    52→          return
    53→        }
    54→        let body = ''
    55→        req.on('data', (chunk: Buffer) => { body += chunk.toString() })
    56→        req.on('end', () => {
    57→          try {
    58→            lastSaveFromBrowser = Date.now()
    59→            fs.writeFileSync(timelineFile, body, 'utf-8')
    60→            res.setHeader('Content-Type', 'application/json')
    61→            res.end(JSON.stringify({ ok: true }))
    62→          } catch (err) {
    63→            res.statusCode = 500
    64→            res.end(JSON.stringify({ error: String(err) }))
    65→          }
    66→        })
    67→      })
    68→
    69→      // Waveform data for audio files (cached)
    70→      server.middlewares.use('/api/waveform', (req, res) => {
    71→        if (req.method !== 'GET') { res.statusCode = 405; res.end(); return }
    72→        const asset = (req.url?.split('?asset=')[1] || '').split('&')[0]
    73→        if (!asset) { res.statusCode = 400; res.end('asset required'); return }
    74→
    75→        // Look up asset path from asset_profiles
    76→        const profilePath = path.join(projectRoot, 'asset_profiles', `${asset}.yaml`)
    77→        if (!fs.existsSync(profilePath)) { res.statusCode = 404; res.end('not found'); return }
    78→
    79→        try {
    80→
    81→          const profile = yaml.load(fs.readFileSync(profilePath, 'utf-8')) as any
    82→
    83→          if (profile.silence_regions) {
    84→            res.setHeader('Content-Type', 'application/json')
    85→            res.end(JSON.stringify({
    86→              asset,
    87→              duration: profile.duration_s,
    88→              silence: profile.silence_regions,
    89→              transcript: profile.transcript?.words || [],
    90→            }))

    93→            res.end('not an audio asset')
    94→          }
    95→        } catch (err) {
    96→          res.statusCode = 500
    97→          res.end(String(err))
    98→        }
    99→      })
   100→

path: \"inputs/my-asset.png\" }\n      server.middlewares.use('/api/asset-map', (req, res) => {\n        if (req.method !== 'PUT') { res.statusCode = 405; res.end(); return }\n        let body = ''\n        req.on('data', (chunk: Buffer) => { body += chunk.toString() })\n        req.on('end', () => {\n          try {\n            const { key, path: assetPath } = JSON.parse(body)\n            if (!assetPath) {\n              res.statusCode = 400\n              res.end(JSON.stringify({ error: 'path is required' }))\n              return\n            }\n            const assetKey = key || assetKeyFromFilename(assetPath.split('/').pop() || assetPath)\n            const { added } = addToAssetMap(assetKey, assetPath, () => { lastSaveFromBrowser = Date.now() })\n            res.setHeader('Content-Type', 'application/json')\n            res.end(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, assetKey, path: assetPath, added }))\n          } catch (err) {\n            res.statusCode = 500\n            res.end(JSON.stringify({ error: String(err) }))\n          }\n        })\n      })\n\n      // Upload media file to inputs/ and register in asset_map\n      server.middlewares.use('/api/upload', (req, res) => {\n        if (req.method !== 'POST') {\n          res.statusCode = 405\n          res.end('Method not allowed')\n          return\n        }\n\n        const filename = decodeURIComponent(req.headers['x-filename'] as string || '')\n        if (!filename || filename.includes('..') || filename.includes('/')) {\n          res.statusCode = 400\n          res.end(JSON.stringify({ error: 'Invalid filename' }))\n          return\n        }\n\n        const inputsDir = path.join(projectRoot, 'inputs')\n        const destPath = path.join(inputsDir, filename)\n\n        const chunks: Buffer[] = []\n        req.on('data', (chunk: Buffer) => chunks.push(chunk))\n        req.on('end', () => {\n          try {\n            fs.writeFileSync(destPath, Buffer.concat(chunks))\n            const [REDACTED](filename)\n            addToAssetMap(assetKey, `inputs/${filename}`, () => { lastSaveFromBrowser = Date.now() })\n            res.setHeader('Content-Type', 'application/json')\n            res.end(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, assetKey, path: `inputs/${filename}` }))\n          } catch (err) {\n            res.statusCode = 500\n            res.end(JSON.stringify({ error: String(err) }))\n          }\n        })\n      })"
}

1→"""
     2→Shared utilities for all video editor tools.
     3→
     4→Importing this module auto-loads .env from the project root.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→import json
     8→import os
     9→import subprocess
    10→from pathlib import Path
    11→
    12→import yaml
    13→
    14→# ── paths ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    15→
    16→ROOT = Path(__file__).parent.parent
    17→PROFILES_DIR = ROOT / "asset_profiles"
    18→TIMELINE_FILE = ROOT / "timeline.yaml"
    19→
    20→# ── auto-load .env ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    21→
    22→_env_file = ROOT / ".env"
    23→if _env_file.exists():
    24→    for _line in _env_file.read_text().splitlines():
    25→        _line = _line.strip()
    26→        if _line and not _line.startswith("#") and "=" in _line:
    27→            _k, _v = _line.split("=", 1)
    28→            os.environ.setdefault(_k.strip(), _v.strip())
    29→
    30→# ── subprocess helpers ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    31→
    32→
    33→def run_cmd(cmd: list[str], **kwargs) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess:
    34→    return subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, **kwargs)
    35→
    36→
    37→def ffprobe_json(path: str) -> dict:
    38→    r = run_cmd(["ffprobe", "-v", "quiet", "-print_format", "json",
    39→                 "-show_format", "-show_streams", path])
    40→    return json.loads(r.stdout)
    41→
    42→
    43→def audio_duration(audio_path: str) -> float:
    44→    """Return duration of audio file in seconds via ffprobe."""
    45→    r = run_cmd(["ffprobe", "-v", "quiet", "-print_format", "json",
    46→                 "-show_format", audio_path])
    47→    return float(json.loads(r.stdout)["format"]["duration"])
    48→
    49→
    50→# ── profile helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    51→
    52→_profile_cache: dict[str, dict] = {}
    53→_profile_paths: dict[str, Path] = {}
    54→
    55→
    56→def load_profile(asset_id: str) -> dict | None:
    57→    if asset_id in _profile_cache:
    58→        return _profile_cache[asset_id]
    59→    for f in PROFILES_DIR.glob("*.yaml"):
    60→        p = yaml.safe_load(f.read_text())
    61→        if p.get("id") == asset_id:
    62→            _profile_cache[asset_id] = p
    63→            _profile_paths[asset_id] = f
    64→            return p
    65→    return None
    66→
    67→
    68→def get_profile_path(asset_id: str) -> Path | None:
    69→    if asset_id not in _profile_paths:
    70→        load_profile(asset_id)
    71→    return _profile_paths.get(asset_id)
    72→
    73→
    74→def load_all_profiles() -> list[dict]:
    75→    profiles = []
    76→    for f in PROFILES_DIR.glob("*.yaml"):
    77→        try:
    78→            p = yaml.safe_load(f.read_text())
    79→            profiles.append(p)
    80→        except Exception:
    81→            pass
    82→    return sorted(profiles, key=lambda p: p.get("file", ""))
    83→
    84→
    85→# ── yaml helpers ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    86→
    87→
    88→def save_yaml(data, path: Path):
    89→    with open(path, "w") as f:
    90→        yaml.dump(data, f, allow_unicode=True, sort_keys=False, default_flow_style=False)
    91→
    92→
    93→# ── openai client ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    94→
    95→
    96→def get_openai_client():
    97→    import openai
    98→    api_key = os.environ.get("OPENAI_API_KEY", "")
    99→    if not api_key:
   100→        return None
   101→    return openai.OpenAI(api_key=api_key)
   102→

I'll add a `register_asset` helper to `common.py` that directly updates `timeline.yaml`, then call it from `imagegen.py`:

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/announcement-video/tools/common.py",
  "old_string": "# ── openai client ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────",
  "new_string": "# ── asset map helpers ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n\ndef register_asset(asset_key: str, asset_path: str) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Add an asset to the timeline's asset_map. Returns True if newly added.\"\"\"\n    timeline = yaml.safe_load(TIMELINE_FILE.read_text())\n    if asset_key in timeline.get(\"asset_map\", {}):\n        return False\n    timeline.setdefault(\"asset_map\", {})[asset_key] = asset_path\n    save_yaml(timeline, TIMELINE_FILE)\n    return True\n\n\n# ── openai client ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────"
}
```

> AGENT

Done. Here's what changed:

1. **Added the 3 reigh images** to `timeline.yaml` `asset_map` — they'll show in the panel now.

2. **`PUT /api/asset-map`** — new endpoint to register any asset. Send JSON like `{ "key": "my-asset", "path": "inputs/my-asset.png" }` (key is optional, auto-derived from filename).

3. **`register_asset()` in `common.py`** — shared Python helper that directly updates `timeline.yaml`'s `asset_map`.

4. **`imagegen.py` auto-registers** — after generating and ingesting, it now calls `register_asset()` so new images appear in the asset panel automatically. No more "Next steps" manual instructions.

5. **Upload endpoint refactored** to use shared `addToAssetMap()` / `assetKeyFromFilename()` helpers in `vite.config.ts`.

I'll add a `/media/thumb/` endpoint to the server that generates and caches compressed JPEG thumbnails on the fly, and have the AssetPanel use those.

> AGENT

Now add the thumbnail endpoint before the render endpoint:

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/announcement-video/viewer/vite.config.ts",
  "old_string": "      // Render preview",
  "new_string": "      // Serve compressed thumbnails: /media/thumb/inputs/foo.png → 400px wide JPEG\n      const thumbDir = path.join(projectRoot, '.thumbs')\n      server.middlewares.use('/media/thumb/', (req, res) => {\n        if (req.method !== 'GET') { res.statusCode = 405; res.end(); return }\n        const relPath = decodeURIComponent((req.url || '').replace(/^\\//, ''))\n        if (!relPath || relPath.includes('..')) { res.statusCode = 400; res.end(); return }\n\n        const srcPath = path.join(projectRoot, relPath)\n        const thumbPath = path.join(thumbDir, relPath.replace(/\\.[^.]+$/, '.jpg'))\n\n        if (!fs.existsSync(srcPath)) { res.statusCode = 404; res.end('not found'); return }\n\n        // Serve cached thumb if fresh\n        try {\n          if (fs.existsSync(thumbPath) && fs.statSync(thumbPath).mtimeMs >= fs.statSync(srcPath).mtimeMs) {\n            res.setHeader('Content-Type', 'image/jpeg')\n            res.setHeader('Cache-Control', 'public, max-age=3600')\n            res.end(fs.readFileSync(thumbPath))\n            return\n          }\n        } catch {}\n\n        // Generate thumb with ffmpeg\n        try {\n          fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(thumbPath), { recursive: true })\n          execFileSync('ffmpeg', [\n            '-i', srcPath,\n            '-vf', 'scale=400:-1',\n            '-q:v', '5',\n            '-y', thumbPath,\n          ], { stdio: 'ignore' })\n          res.setHeader('Content-Type', 'image/jpeg')\n          res.setHeader('Cache-Control', 'public, max-age=3600')\n          res.end(fs.readFileSync(thumbPath))\n        } catch {\n          res.statusCode = 500\n          res.end('thumbnail generation failed')\n        }\n      })\n\n      // Render preview"
}
```

> AGENT

Done. Here's what changed:

- **New `/media/thumb/` endpoint** — generates 400px-wide JPEG thumbnails on the fly using ffmpeg, caches them in `.thumbs/`. Subsequent requests serve the cached version instantly. A 5-6MB PNG becomes a ~20-30KB JPEG.
- **Asset panel image previews** now load from `/media/thumb/` instead of the full-size original.
- Thumbnails auto-regenerate if the source file is newer than the cached version.

You'll need to restart the dev server. Add `.thumbs/` to `.gitignore` if you want to keep it out of version control.

> DEVELOPER

How can I make that into an app? Is there like some kind of way that I can so that the thing shows up in Chrome that I can actually just open it as an app in the Mac dashboard?

> AGENT

You can use Chrome's "Install as app" feature:

1. Open **http://localhost:5173** in Chrome
2. Click the **three dots menu** (top right) → **"Save and Share"** → **"Install Page as App..."**
3. It'll appear in your Mac dock/Launchpad as a standalone window

That's the quickest way. It works because the viewer already has a basic HTML page that Chrome can wrap as a PWA-like app window.

     2→import type { TimelineRow } from '@xzdarcy/react-timeline-editor'

     4→
     5→interface AssetPanelProps {

     7→  rows: TimelineRow[]
     8→  meta: Record<string, ClipMeta>
     9→  backgroundAsset?: string
    10→}
    11→
    12→const ACCEPTED_EXTENSIONS = [
    13→  '.mp4', '.webm', '.mov',
    14→  '.mp3', '.wav', '.aac', '.m4a',
    15→  '.jpg', '.jpeg', '.png', '.gif', '.svg',
    16→]
    17→
    18→function isMediaFile(file: File): boolean {
    19→  const ext = file.name.slice(file.name.lastIndexOf('.')).toLowerCase()
    20→  return ACCEPTED_EXTENSIONS.includes(ext)
    21→}
    22→
    23→export default function AssetPanel({ assetMap, rows, meta, backgroundAsset }: AssetPanelProps) {
    24→  const [showAll, setShowAll] = useState(false)
    25→  const [hoveredAsset, setHoveredAsset] = useState<{ key: string; path: string; trackType: string } | null>(null)
    26→  const [fileDragOver, setFileDragOver] = useState(false)
    27→  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false)
    28→  const videoRef = useRef<HTMLVideoElement>(null)
    29→  const dragCounter = useRef(0)
    30→
    31→  const usedAssets = useMemo(() => {
    32→    const used = new Set<string>()
    33→    for (const row of rows) {
    34→      for (const action of row.actions) {
    35→        const m = meta[action.id]
    36→        if (m) used.add(m.asset)
    37→      }
    38→    }
    39→    return used
    40→  }, [rows, meta])
    41→
    42→  const assets = useMemo(() => {
    43→    return Object.entries(assetMap)
    44→      .filter(([key]) => key !== backgroundAsset)
    45→      .filter(([key]) => showAll || !usedAssets.has(key))
    46→      .map(([key, path]) => ({
    47→        key,
    48→        path,
    49→        trackType: inferTrackType(path),
    50→      }))
    51→  }, [assetMap, backgroundAsset, showAll, usedAssets])
    52→
    53→  // When hovered asset changes to a video, play it
    54→  useEffect(() => {
    55→    if (hoveredAsset?.trackType === 'video' && videoRef.current) {
    56→      videoRef.current.currentTime = 0
    57→      videoRef.current.play().catch(() => {})
    58→    }
    59→  }, [hoveredAsset])
    60→
    61→  const typeLabel = (t: string) => {
    62→    if (t === 'video') return 'VIDEO'
    63→    if (t === 'audio') return 'AUDIO'
    64→    return 'IMAGE'
    65→  }
    66→
    67→  const typeBadgeClass = (t: string) => `asset-type-badge asset-type-${t}`
    68→
    69→  const onDragStart = (e: React.DragEvent, key: string, trackType: string) => {
    70→    e.dataTransfer.setData('asset-key', key)
    71→    e.dataTransfer.setData('track-type', trackType)
    72→    e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = 'copy'
    73→  }
    74→
    75→  // File drop handling
    76→  const hasFiles = (e: React.DragEvent) => e.dataTransfer.types.includes('Files')
    77→
    78→  const onFileDragEnter = useCallback((e: React.DragEvent) => {
    79→    if (!hasFiles(e)) return
    80→    e.preventDefault()
    81→    dragCounter.current++
    82→    setFileDragOver(true)
    83→  }, [])
    84→
    85→  const onFileDragOver = useCallback((e: React.DragEvent) => {
    86→    if (!hasFiles(e)) return
    87→    e.preventDefault()
    88→    e.dataTransfer.dropEffect = 'copy'
    89→  }, [])
    90→
    91→  const onFileDragLeave = useCallback((e: React.DragEvent) => {
    92→    if (!hasFiles(e)) return
    93→    e.preventDefault()
    94→    dragCounter.current--
    95→    if (dragCounter.current <= 0) {
    96→      dragCounter.current = 0
    97→      setFileDragOver(false)
    98→    }
    99→  }, [])
   100→
   101→  const uploadFile = useCallback(async (file: File) => {
   102→    const res = await fetch('/api/upload', {
   103→      method: 'POST',
   104→      headers: { 'X-Filename': encodeURIComponent(file.name) },
   105→      body: file,
   106→    })
   107→    if (!res.ok) {
   108→      const err = await res.json()
   109→      throw new Error(err.error || 'Upload failed')
   110→    }
   111→    return res.json()
   112→  }, [])
   113→
   114→  const onFileDrop = useCallback(async (e: React.DragEvent) => {
   115→    e.preventDefault()
   116→    dragCounter.current = 0
   117→    setFileDragOver(false)
   118→
   119→    const files = Array.from(e.dataTransfer.files).filter(isMediaFile)
   120→    if (files.length === 0) return

   122→    setUploading(true)

1. **Asset Panel with Drag-Drop to Timeline** - The initial request was to implement a plan for adding a right-side asset panel with drag-drop support. This involved:

   - Adding drop handlers on timeline

   - Fixing `applyTimelineEdit` to support new clips

2. **Asset hover preview** - User requested showing a preview when hovering over assets in the panel. Added video/image/audio previews.

3. **Delete buttons on timeline clips** - Added `×` delete buttons on each clip in the timeline, with `handleDeleteClip` function.

4. **Delete button positioning** - User said button shows over edge if item too small, wanted it in top-right corner. Changed from flex layout to `position: absolute; top: 2px; right: 2px`.

5. **Timeline scrolling** - User couldn't scroll right along timeline. Fixed by changing `overflow: visible !important` to `overflow: auto !important` on `.timeline-editor-edit-area` and its `ReactVirtualized__Grid`.

6. **Zoom controls** - Added zoom in/out buttons, `scaleCount` computation, dynamic `scale`/`scaleWidth`.

7. **Code quality review** - User asked if code was well-structured. Identified 3 issues:
   - `handleDeleteClip` bypassing `applyTimelineEdit` for meta
   - `data.effects` mutated directly in `handleAssetDrop`
   - `scale` stateful but never changes
   Fixed all three.

8. **Video stop at end** - User wanted playback to stop after last clip. Added `timelineEnd` computation and auto-pause in `tick()` loop in `LivePreview.tsx`.

9. **File drag-drop upload** - User wanted to drag files from OS onto asset panel. Added `/api/upload` endpoint in `vite.config.ts` and drop zone UI in `AssetPanel.tsx`.

10. **Upload error fix** - `require('js-yaml')` failed in Vite ESM context. Fixed by using top-level `import yaml from 'js-yaml'`.

11. **Frame extraction** - Extracted frames from videos using ffmpeg.

12. **Image generation with imagegen** - Generated multiple background images for Reigh branding using the palette icon concept. Many iterations.

13. **Auto-register assets** - User noticed generated images didn't show in asset panel because they weren't in `asset_map`. Added:

14. **Background image changes** - Multiple iterations changing the `background` field in `timeline.yaml`.

15. **Many image generation iterations** - The user went through many rounds of generating and refining background images with different styles, angles, compositions.

16. **Thumbnail optimization** - User noticed slow loading of large PNG files (5-6MB each). Added `/media/thumb/` endpoint that generates cached 400px JPEG thumbnails via ffmpeg.

17. **Hide assets feature** - User wanted to hide assets from the panel. Added:

    - Hide/unhide buttons on each asset
    - "N hidden" toggle button in header
    - Hidden assets shown dimmed when revealed

2. Key Technical Concepts:

   - YAML-based timeline (`timeline.yaml`) with asset_map, clips, output settings
   - Vite dev server custom middleware plugins for API endpoints
   - HTML5 drag-and-drop API (both internal asset drag-to-timeline and OS file drop)
   - `ReactVirtualized` grid overflow handling for timeline scrolling
   - ffmpeg for thumbnail generation and frame extraction

3. Files and Code Sections:

   - **`viewer/src/timeline-data.ts`**
     - Added `inferTrackType()` and `getNextClipId()` utilities for drag-drop
     ```typescript
     export function inferTrackType(filePath: string): 'video' | 'audio' | 'overlay' {
       const ext = filePath.slice(filePath.lastIndexOf('.')).toLowerCase()
       if (['.mp4', '.webm', '.mov'].includes(ext)) return 'video'
       if (['.mp3', '.wav', '.aac', '.m4a'].includes(ext)) return 'audio'
       return 'overlay'
     }

     export function getNextClipId(meta: Record<string, ClipMeta>): string {
       let max = -1
       for (const id of Object.keys(meta)) {
         const match = id.match(/^clip-(\d+)$/)
         if (match) max = Math.max(max, parseInt(match[1], 10))
       }
       return `clip-${max + 1}`
     }
     ```

   - **`viewer/src/AssetPanel.tsx`** (NEW)
     - Draggable asset list with hover previews, file upload drop zone, hide/unhide functionality
     - Uses localStorage to persist hidden assets
     - Shows video/image/audio previews on hover (images use `/media/thumb/` for compressed thumbnails)
     - Current state: hide/show buttons use text labels `'hide'`/`'show'` (changed from `×`/`○` which looked like delete button)

   - **`viewer/src/App.tsx`**
     - Layout restructured: `.video-container` now contains `.preview-section` (LivePreview + OverlayEditor) + `AssetPanel`
     - `applyTimelineEdit` extended with `metaDeletes` parameter for clip deletion:
     ```typescript
     const applyTimelineEdit = useCallback((
       newRows: TimelineRow[],
       metaUpdates?: Record<string, Partial<ClipMeta>>,
       metaDeletes?: string[],
     ) => { ... })
     ```
     - `handleAssetDrop` creates new clips with defaults per track type, finds/creates rows
     - `handleDeleteClip` now goes through `applyTimelineEdit` instead of calling `setMeta` directly
     - Effects updated immutably via `setData()` instead of mutating `data.effects`
     - `scale` changed from state to constant `const scale = 5`
     - Added `scaleCount` (computed from max clip end time), zoom controls (`handleZoomIn`/`handleZoomOut`), drop handlers on `.timeline-wrapper`
     - Clip delete button in `getActionRender` with `position: absolute; top: 2px; right: 2px`

   - **`viewer/src/App.css`**
     - Added `.preview-section` wrapper styles
     - Full asset panel styling (`.asset-panel`, `.asset-list`, `.asset-item`, type badges, preview area)
     - Clip delete button: absolutely positioned top-right, appears on hover, turns red
     - Zoom controls styling
     - Asset dropzone overlay, uploading indicator
     - Asset hide button: appears on hover, text-based
     - Timeline `.drag-over` outline indicator
     - Changed `.timeline-editor-edit-area` and `.ReactVirtualized__Grid` from `overflow: visible !important` to `overflow: auto !important` to fix scrolling

   - **`viewer/src/LivePreview.tsx`**
     - Added `timelineEnd` computation and auto-pause in `tick()`:
     ```typescript
     const timelineEnd = useMemo(() => {
       let maxEnd = 0
       for (const clip of clips) {
         if (clip.end > maxEnd) maxEnd = clip.end
       }
       return maxEnd
     }, [clips])
     ```
     - Added `timelineEndRef` and `pauseRef` for RAF loop access
     - `tick()` checks `time >= timelineEndRef.current` and calls `pauseRef.current()`

   - **`viewer/vite.config.ts`**
     - Added `import yaml from 'js-yaml'` (replaced `require('js-yaml')` which failed in ESM)
     - Extracted `addToAssetMap()` and `assetKeyFromFilename()` helper functions
     - Added `PUT /api/asset-map` endpoint for registering assets
     - Added `POST /api/upload` endpoint for file uploads (saves to `inputs/`, auto-registers in asset_map)
     - Added `/media/thumb/` endpoint that generates cached 400px JPEG thumbnails via ffmpeg in `.thumbs/` directory

   - **`tools/common.py`**
     - Added `register_asset(asset_key, asset_path)` helper that updates `timeline.yaml`

   - **`tools/imagegen.py`**
     - Replaced "Next steps" manual instructions with auto-registration via `register_asset()`

   - **`timeline.yaml`**
     - Many background assets added to `asset_map` through iterations
     - Current background: `reigh-bg-v17-titled-dust`
     - Many `reigh-*` assets registered

4. Errors and fixes:
   - **Unused import `inferTrackType` in App.tsx**: Removed the import (only needed in AssetPanel)
   - **Implicit `any` type on `.some(a =>)`**: Added explicit type annotation `(a: TimelineAction) =>`
   - **`overflow: visible !important` killing timeline scroll**: Changed to `overflow: auto !important` on `.timeline-editor-edit-area` and `.ReactVirtualized__Grid`
   - **`width: 100% !important` on timeline editor**: First changed to `min-width: 100%; width: max-content`, then reverted to `width: 100% !important` since the library handles its own sizing internally with scaleCount
   - **`require('js-yaml')` failing in Vite ESM context** (`Dynamic require of "file:///...js-yaml/dist/js-yaml.mjs" is not supported`): Replaced with top-level `import yaml from 'js-yaml'`
   - **ffmpeg frame extraction failures**: Used `-update 1` flag for single image output, used `-sseof -0.1` for webm files where seeking past duration failed
   - **fal.ai 500 error** (service temporarily overloaded): Retried after short delay
   - **Delete button overlapping clip edges**: Changed from flex layout to `position: absolute; top: 2px; right: 2px` in top-right corner
   - **Hide button looking like delete button**: User said "that looks just like the delete button" — changed from `×`/`○` symbols to `hide`/`show` text labels

5. Problem Solving:
   - Timeline scrolling fixed by restoring library's internal overflow handling
   - Code quality: centralized all edits through `applyTimelineEdit`, fixed state mutation, removed dead state
   - Video playback: added end-of-timeline detection with auto-pause
   - Asset discovery: created pipeline from imagegen → register_asset → asset_map → viewer
   - Performance: added thumbnail caching endpoint to avoid loading 5-6MB PNGs for previews
   - Many iterations on Reigh background images trying to balance palette visibility, orbital feel, subtlety
